Google Calendar help

This guide holds Google’s own help for Google Calendar, gathered from support.google.com on 14 November 2026. It covers calendars, events, appointment schedules, sharing, settings and Tasks in Calendar, on a computer and on an iPhone or iPad. Using Calendar with a screen reader is under Get started.

Google writes the same article once for each device it runs on, so where an article differs by platform the heading says which one it is for: Computer, or iPhone and iPad. Google’s Android editions are left out, as this collection covers Windows, the web, iPhone and iPad, and Alexa. The categories are Google’s own topics: Google no longer puts a trail above each article, so each article is filed under the topic its help centre lists it in.

The help text belongs to its publisher and is gathered here unchanged for accessible reading. This guide’s own arrangement and wording are available under CC BY-SA 4.0.

Add attachments, rooms, or video conferencing to an event

Add a room to an event

Important: To add meeting rooms and other resources to your event, you need to have a work or school account. If you have a personal Google Account that ends in @gmail.com, learn how to create a calendar for a room or shared space.

You can add meeting rooms and other resources, like projectors, to your event. If you can’t book a room, learn why a Google Calendar meeting room declines an event.

Google Calendar can check for room conflicts up to 12 months in advance. If you schedule events or recurring instances more than 12 months out, Google Calendar won’t detect conflicts in these.

Add a room to your event

Important: If you can’t add a room to an event, Google Calendar may suggest a shortcut to create a duplicate event where you can book a room. This shortcut creates a single event, without any guests.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Create a new event or open an existing one.

    • To edit an existing event, click Edit event .

  • If your account is part of an organization with Rooms, click Add rooms or Add room booking.

    • Based on who you invite, you can choose from suggested rooms or search for a new one.
    • Click the rooms or resources you want to add.

  • Click Save. Tips:

  • You can book more than one room for an event.

  • If you add a room but its schedule is missing, in the scheduling grid, click Filter and view Show rooms. Learn how to find times to meet in Google Calendar.

  • If you’re an administrator, learn how to create rooms in your Admin Console.

Remove a room from your event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open an existing event.
  • At the top right, click Edit event .
  • In the “Rooms” list, find the room or resource.
  • Next to the room or resource, click Remove room .
  • Click Save.

Related resources

  • Learn why a Google Calendar meeting room declines an event
  • Create a calendar for a room or shared space

Add a room to an event (Computer)

Important: To add meeting rooms and other resources to your event, you need to have a work or school account. If you have a personal Google Account that ends in @gmail.com, learn how to create a calendar for a room or shared space.

You can add meeting rooms and other resources, like projectors, to your event. If you can’t book a room, learn why a Google Calendar meeting room declines an event.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

Add a room to an event (iPhone and iPad)

Important: To add meeting rooms and other resources to your event, you need to have a work or school account. If you have a personal Google Account that ends in @gmail.com, learn how to create a calendar for a room or shared space.

You can add meeting rooms and other resources, like projectors, to your event. If you can’t book a room, learn why a Google Calendar meeting room declines an event.

Google Calendar can check for room conflicts up to 12 months in advance. If you schedule events or recurring instances more than 12 months out, Google Calendar won’t detect conflicts in these.

Add a room to your event

  • Open the Calendar app .

  • Create a new event or open an existing one.

    • To edit an existing event, tap Edit event .

  • Tap Add room.

  • Select a room.

  • Tap Save. Tips:

  • You can book more than one room for an event.

  • If you’re an administrator, you can add new rooms and resources in the Google Admin console.

Remove a room from your event

  • Open the Calendar app .
  • To change the event, open the event and tap Edit event .
  • Tap the room Remove .
  • Tap Save.

Related resources

  • Learn why a Google Calendar meeting room declines an event
  • Create a calendar for a room or shared space

Add attachments to your events

To give your guests important event information, you can add documents, spreadsheets, meeting notes, and other files directly to an event.

Add an attachment

You can add files from Google Drive or upload files from your computer.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Create an event, or open an existing event.
  • If you create a new event, click More options.
  • At the bottom, in the description box, click Add a Google Drive attachment .
  • Choose how to add your file:
    • From Google Drive: Select a file and click Insert.
    • From your computer:
      • Click Upload, and drag and drop your file into the window,
      • Or, click Browse to choose a file from your computer.
  • At the top of the event window, click Save.

Sharing options

If you’ve invited other people to the event and they don’t have access to view the file, you’ll be asked to choose one of these sharing options when you save the event:

  • Anyone with the link can view, comment, or edit: Anyone who has the link can access the file. Guests can share the file with someone else, and they won’t need a Google Account to see it.

  • Guests of this event can view, comment, or edit: Shares the file with your guests. They need a Google Account to see the file. They can only share the file with someone else if you give them “Can edit” access.

  • Save without sharing: Guests who don’t have access will see the title of the file, but they won’t be able to open it. Tips:

  • You can also open the file in the Google Drive app for other sharing options.

  • The names of any files you attach to your event will be visible to both invited guests and anyone with access to the event details.

Remove an attachment

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open an event.
  • In the description box, hover over the attachment you want to remove.
  • Click Remove attachment .
  • At the top, click Save.

Add meeting notes to events

You can start and share meeting notes directly from Google Calendar events.

Important: You must be on a computer to add meeting notes to an event.

Add meeting notes to a new event from Google Calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Create an event.
  • Optional: Enter the title, time, guests, and other details.
  • Click Add description or a Google Drive attachment Create meeting notes Save. Important: To create meeting notes, you need a work or school account. If you use a personal Google Account, you can manually attach Google Docs or files from Google Drive.

Add attachments & notes to an existing event from Google Calendar

Important: To add meeting notes to an existing event, you must have permission to modify the event.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an event and select an option:
    • Start a new meeting notes document for work or school accounts: In the event preview pop-up, click Take meeting notes. Pre-populated meeting notes with event details are attached to your event.
    • Attach existing meeting notes: Next to “Take meeting notes”, click More meeting note options Attach existing notes select your file from Google Drive and click Insert.
  • Selected meeting notes are attached to your event.
  • Meeting notes open in a new window. On the right, click Share.

Add meeting notes to events from Google Docs

  • On your computer, open a new or existing Google Doc you want to add to an event.
  • In the doc, type “@”.
  • In the pop-up menu, click Meeting notes.
  • Search for an event.
    • Tip: You can type “next” to select the next meeting on your calendar.
  • Select an event. Meeting notes are pre-populated with event details but aren’t attached to the event.
    • If you’re the meeting organizer: A pop-up prompts you to share and attach the document to your event. To give access, click Share & attach.
    • If you’re not the meeting organizer: A pop-up prompts you to share the document. To give access, click Share. The document doesn’t attach to the event.

Learn about Meeting notes

  • When you add Meeting notes to an event, the changes don’t appear in Google Calendar and vice versa. For example:
    • If you update a document’s attendee list with new guests, the new guests aren’t automatically invited to the event.
    • If you change the document’s title, the title doesn’t change in Google Calendar.
    • If you change an event in Google Calendar, event details don’t change in the Meeting notes.
  • You can’t add meeting notes to an event if:
    • You don’t have permission to edit the event.
    • Another person already attached meeting notes.

Troubleshooting

Guests don’t have permission to find attachments

Attachments are stored in Google Drive, so your guests don’t automatically have permission to view your attachments. To give your guests access to the files, you can share each file from Google Drive.

“Attachments can’t be added because Google Drive is disabled by your domain administrator” message

If your work, school, or organization turns off Drive service, you get this message: “Attachments can’t be added because Google Drive is disabled by your domain administrator.” If you find this message, contact your admin.

I can’t find the option to add a Google Drive attachment

If your Google Drive isn’t linked to the Google Account you use to create the event, you won’t find the option to add an attachment from Google Drive.

To fix this, make sure:

  • The documents you want to attach upload to Google Drive.
  • You sign in with the same account that you use for Google Drive.

Add attachments to your events (iPhone and iPad)

To give your guests important event information, you can add documents, spreadsheets, meeting notes, and other files directly to an event.

Add an attachment

You can add files and documents from Google Drive directly to your event.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • Create a new event, or edit an existing event.
  • Tap Add Google Drive attachment.
  • Tap an item in your Google Drive Done .

Sharing options

If you’ve invited other people to the event and they don’t have access to view the file, you’ll be asked to choose one of these sharing options when you save the event:

  • Anyone with the link can view, comment, or edit: Anyone who has the link can access the file. Guests can share the file with someone else, and they won’t need a Google Account to see it.

  • You can also open the file in the Google Drive app for other sharing options.

  • The names of any files you attach to your event will be visible to both invited guests and anyone with access to the event details.

Remove an attachment

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • On the event with the attachment to remove, tap Edit .
  • Tap the attachment.
  • Tap Remove Save.

Guests don’t have permission to find attachments

If your work, school, or organization turns off Drive service, you get this message: “Attachments can’t be added because Google Drive is disabled by your domain administrator.” If you find this message, contact your admin.

I can’t find the option to add a Google Drive attachment

If your Google Drive isn’t linked to the Google Account you use to create the event, you won’t find the option to add an attachment from Google Drive.

To fix this, make sure:

  • The documents you want to attach upload to Google Drive.
  • You sign in with the same account that you use for Google Drive.

Add or remove a video conference from your Calendar event

You can add or remove a video conference to your events in Google Calendar. You can use Google Meet or another video conferencing add-on.

Add Google Meet video meetings to Calendar events

Change video conference option on an event

Important: When you add Google Meet to a Google Calendar event, you get a unique meeting code. Until it expires, the meeting code is permanently linked to the original Calendar event. The guest list for the original event controls who can join your meeting and who has access to meeting records, like recordings and chat history. Learn about meeting codes in Google Calendar events.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Create an event or open an existing event you have edit access to.
  • In the event, find the conferencing field.
    • To add a new conference, click Add Google Meet video conferencing.
    • To remove an existing video conference, click Remove .
    • If you installed a conferencing add-on from the Google Workspace Marketplace, select the conferencing provider from the “Add conferencing” dropdown menu. Learn how to install an add-on from the Marketplace.
  • Click Save.

Take notes with Gemini in Meet

Important:

  • This feature requires an eligible Google Workspace plan. Learn about Gemini features and plans.

  • To turn on this feature from an event, your Calendar language must be set to English.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • At the top left, click Create Event.

  • Click Add Google Meet video conferencing.

  • Turn on Use Gemini to take meeting notes.

  • Add guests and other details to your event.

  • Click Save. Tips:

  • Only event organizers can turn on Gemini to take meeting notes.

  • To turn off this feature for your meeting:

    • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
    • Edit the event.
    • Click Manage settings.
    • Deselect Take notes with Gemini and Transcribe the meeting.
    • Click Save. Learn how to take notes with Gemini in Meet.

Troubleshooting

I want to use a different conferencing solution

Important: If you have a work or school account, your administrator controls the add-ons that you can use or install in Calendar.

You can install a video conferencing add-on to use with Calendar. After installation, you can manually add video conferencing to your events. Learn how to find and install an add-on for Calendar.

I want to automatically add a Google Meet video conference to my event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left, click Event settings.
  • Turn on Automatically add Google Meet video conferences to events I create.

I want to avoid multiple conferences on single events

  • An event can have only one conference. You can use Google Meet or another video conferencing add-on.
  • To use another video conference add-on, find and install the add-on from the Marketplace.
  • When you add a conference to your event, select the add-on in the conferencing field dropdown.
  • If you insert conference details directly into the location field or event description, or use a browser extension, it may result in multiple conferences being attached to a single event.

Learn about meeting codes in Google Calendar events

When you add Google Meet to a Google Calendar event, you get a unique meeting code. Until it expires, the meeting code is permanently linked to the original Calendar event. The guest list for the original event controls who can join your meeting and who has access to meeting records, like recordings and chat history. Learn more about meeting codes in Google Meet.

If you reuse a meeting code from an old event for a new meeting, the permissions and access are still tied to the original event’s guest list. This means that:

  • Sensitive discussions, like performance reviews or interviews, could be visible to people invited to the original event.
  • People invited to the original event might have access to recordings and other files from the new meeting.
  • If continuous meeting chat is enabled, participants from the original event can receive chat messages sent in the new meeting, even if they aren’t invited to the event. Learn more about continuous meeting chat.
  • Guests invited to the new event may need to ask permission to join the event from the host. Example

A recruiter uses the same meeting code for a candidate interview and a separate internal follow-up debrief with the hiring team. Because the code still links to the interview event:

  • The hiring team has to ask to join the meeting from the recruiter and does not have access to the chat before and after the meeting.
  • The candidate might find the follow-up meeting’s notes and recordings. To avoid sharing notes with the candidate and allow the hiring team to automatically join the meeting, the recruiter should create a new event with a unique meeting code for the follow-up meeting.

If you reuse a meeting code that was created outside of Calendar, such as meet.google.com:

  • Continuous meeting chat is not available for the meeting.
  • Meeting records can only be shared with the meeting host.
  • People invited to the event may need to ask permission to join the event from the host.

Learn best practices for meeting codes

  • Create a new meeting code: Always create a new code when you schedule a new event. When you add Meet to a new Calendar event, it generates a new meeting code.
    • For recurring events, the code connects to the event in progress or the next upcoming event.
  • Do not reuse meeting codes to hide guests: If you need to keep attendee names private, create one event and select “Hide guest list” in the event permissions:
    • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
    • Open the event.
    • Go to the “Guests” tab.
    • Under “Guest permissions,” uncheck the box next to “See guest list.”

Understand meeting code warnings

If you see a warning that says “The meeting code is from a different meeting” and you don’t know why or don’t know what the other meeting is, you should generate a new meeting code.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open the event.
  • At the top right, click Edit event .
  • On the right of the current video conference, click Remove conference .
  • Click Save.
  • Open the event.
  • Click Edit event .
  • To generate a new meeting code unique to the event, click Add Google Meet video conferencing. Tip: For the best experience, use Google Meet with Google Calendar. If you create Google Calendar events with Google Meet codes in Apple Calendar or other calendar apps, a new meeting code might be generated automatically to ensure the privacy and security of your meeting. You will receive an email notification if this happens. To manage meeting links, use the Google Calendar app for iPhone or iPad, or go to calendar.google.com.

Related resources

  • Add or remove a video conference from your Calendar event
  • Learn about meeting codes in Google Meet

Learn why a Google Calendar meeting room declines an event

Important: To add meeting rooms and other resources to your event, you need to have a work or school account. Learn how to add a room to an event.

A Google Calendar meeting room may decline an event for various reasons, either at the time of booking or afterward.

Tips:

  • When a room booking is declined, the event organizer receives an email. Guests don’t receive an email but can see the room acceptance status in the event.
  • If the event is on a secondary calendar, no one receives an email but you can find the room acceptance or decline in the event.
  • If you update an event with a booked room, check if the room is available for the new time or book a new room.
  • If you update an event’s time, the booked room is released, and attempts are made to rebook it automatically for the new time. An automatic rebooking may fail for the same reasons as a new room booking.
  • For some work or school accounts, if your room’s declined, your administrator can turn on a setting to automatically book a similar room.
    • For events with fewer than 20 guests, the organizer and guests receive an email with new room details.
    • For more than 20 guests, only the organizer and the event creator receive an email with new room details.
  • If you create an event for a time that has already passed and add a meeting room or resource, the room doesn’t automatically accept the invitation.

Understand the reasons for a room decline

Unavailable room at the requested time

If the room has already been booked for another event that occurs at the same time, the room declines the request immediately. Rooms can decline events if:

  • A user updates an event with a booked room but the room isn’t available at the new time.
  • Another user tries to book the room for a separate event at the same time. Unavailable room for a recurring event

For a recurring event, make sure the room’s available for at least half of the events, and isn’t unavailable more than 8 times.

Tip: To avoid this conflict, when you edit a recurring event, under “Rooms,” select Rooms for future events.

Learn how to add a room to an event.

All but one attendee decline the event

If your administrator turns on room releases and all attendees except one decline the event, the room is automatically released. Learn more about how your administrator can release unused Calendar meeting rooms.

If you don’t decline the event, it’s highlighted. The event highlight disappears if:

  • Another guest changes their RSVP to “Maybe” or “Yes.”
  • A new guest is added. You don’t have permission to book the room

If you don’t have permission, in the room selector, the room shows as unavailable.

The room declines if:

  • You update the event time after the room administrator removes your permission.

  • A guest without booking permission changes the event time. If you need to book the room, ask your administrator to:

  • Change the sharing options for the room to “See event details.”

  • Give you permission to book the room. A resource manager removes the room

Resource managers can remove a room booking even if no changes are made by the event organizer or guests. Resource managers have these permissions:

  • “Make changes to events” permission: Remove rooms from existing events.
  • “Make changes and manage sharing” permission: Delete the resource calendar. Only your administrator can set up a resource manager.

Resources in different time zones can’t be booked for all-day events

When you add a room or resource to an all-day event, Google Calendar schedules it in Coordinated Universal Time (UTC). The event runs from midnight to midnight the next day, regardless of the room’s time zone. This can cause the resource to decline the event, even if the calendar shows the resource is available.

Tip: To book a resource in a different time zone, schedule the event for 24 hours. Do not select All day.

Related resources

  • Add a room to an event
  • Approve or deny Calendar room & resource bookings
  • Release unused Calendar meeting rooms
  • Share room and resource calendars

Create & subscribe to new calendars

Add or remove alternate calendars

You can add different types of calendars, including lunar or solar, Moon phase, and sports. To add or remove alternate calendars, update your calendar settings.

There are several calendars available, like:

  • Lunar: Based on the Moon’s phases.
  • Solar: Based on the Sun’s annual cycles.
  • Moon phases: Based on the primary moon phases: New Moon, First Quarter, Full Moon, and Last Quarter.
  • Sports: A schedule of upcoming sports games. You can add sports calendars for baseball, basketball, cricket, football, hockey, or rugby.

Add or remove lunar and solar calendars

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • Click View options.
  • Under “General,” click Alternate calendars drop-down.
  • Select your preferred calendar. Tip: To remove an alternate calendar, select None.

Add or remove Moon phases

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • Under “Add calendar,” click Browse calendars of interest.
  • Check or uncheck the box next to “Phases of the Moon.” Add a Moon phase calendar

Add or remove sports calendars

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • Under “Add calendar,” click Browse calendars of interest.
  • Scroll to the “Sports” section.
  • Select a sports category.
  • Check or uncheck the box next to a sports team to add their game schedule to your calendar. Add a sports calendar

Related resources

  • Change your Calendar settings
  • Add or remove holidays on your calendar
  • Manage birthdays on your calendar

Add or remove alternate calendars (iPhone and iPad)

You can add different types of calendars, including lunar or solar, Moon phase, and sports. To add or remove alternate calendars, update your calendar settings.

Add or remove lunar and solar calendars

Important: Some calendars, like sports or moon phases, can only be set up on your computer. When you enable these types of calendars, they appear on your mobile device.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings.
  • Tap General Alternate calendars.
  • Select your preferred calendar. Tip: To remove an alternate calendar, in “Alternate calendars,” tap No alternate calendar.

Related resources

  • Change your Calendar settings
  • Add or remove holidays on your calendar
  • Manage birthdays on your calendar

Add or remove holidays on your calendar

Important: To learn more about changes on how Calendar shows holidays and other moments, check The Keyword blog.

Holidays are automatically added to your calendar. You can update your calendar settings to:

  • Show public holidays and other observances, public holidays only, or selected holidays.
  • Add or remove holidays from other countries or regions.
  • Add or remove religious holidays.
  • Hide or unhide individual holidays.
  • Change the color of holiday events. To show the holidays most important to you, you can customize your calendar.

Tips:

  • Calendar shows public holidays and national observances using information from a third-party company that specializes in this data. This information is regularly updated and available for every country where Calendar can be used.
  • To add specific holidays, you can subscribe to third-party holiday calendars published on the web.

Select holidays to show on your calendar

Important: You can’t edit the details of individual holidays on your calendar.

Public holidays and other observances appear on your calendar by default. To show public holidays only:

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • Under “Regional holidays,” find the holiday calendars you’re subscribed to.
  • Next to your preferred holiday calendar, click the drop-down.
  • To change your preferred holidays, select the holidays you want to show on your calendar:
    • Public holidays
    • Other observances Change holiday settings

Add or remove country and region holidays

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • Under “Regional holidays,” click Browse all.
  • To add or remove a calendar, check the box next to it.

Add or remove religious holidays

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • To add or remove a religious holiday, under “Global religious holidays,” check or uncheck the box next to it. Tip: To preview religious holidays, click Preview .

Hide all holidays from your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “Other calendars,” uncheck Holidays. Tip: Calendars with an empty box are already hidden from your calendar.

Hide individual holidays from your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Select a holiday.
  • Click Trash Hide the holiday.

Unhide individual holidays from your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • Next to your preferred holiday calendar, click Expand .
  • Select specific holidays and observances you want to show on your calendar.

Subscribe to holiday calendars published on the web

You can use a link to add many third-party holiday calendars published on the web to Google Calendar.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, next to “Other calendars,” click Add other calendars From URL.
  • Enter the URL of the published calendar.
  • Click Add calendar.
    • The calendar displays on the left, under “Other calendars.”

Change the color of holiday events

  • Open Google Calendar.

  • On the left, find the holiday calendar.

  • Click Options .

  • Select a new color. Tips:

  • The color applies to all holiday events on your calendar.

  • You can’t individually change the color of holiday events.

Related resources

  • Change Google Calendar settings
  • Use labels to track calendar entries

Holidays are automatically added to your calendar. You can update your calendar settings to:

  • Show public holidays and other observances, public holidays only, or selected holidays.
  • Add or remove holidays from other countries or regions.
  • Add or remove religious holidays.
  • Hide or unhide individual holidays.
  • Change the color of holiday events. To show the holidays most important to you, you can customize your calendar.

Tips:

  • Calendar shows public holidays and national observances using information from a third-party company that specializes in this data. This information is regularly updated and available for every country where Calendar can be used.
  • To add specific holidays, you can subscribe to third-party holiday calendars published on the web.

Select holidays to show on your calendar

Important: You can’t edit the details of individual holidays on your calendar.

Public holidays and other observances appear on your calendar by default. To show public holidays only:

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • Under “Regional holidays,” find the holiday calendars you’re subscribed to.
  • Next to your preferred holiday calendar, click the drop-down.
  • To change your preferred holidays, select the holidays you want to show on your calendar:
    • Public holidays
    • Other observances Change holiday settings

Add or remove country and region holidays

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • Under “Regional holidays,” click Browse all.
  • To add or remove a calendar, check the box next to it.

Add or remove religious holidays

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • To add or remove a religious holiday, under “Global religious holidays,” check or uncheck the box next to it. Tip: To preview religious holidays, click Preview .

Hide all holidays from your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “Other calendars,” uncheck Holidays. Tip: Calendars with an empty box are already hidden from your calendar.

Hide individual holidays from your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Select a holiday.
  • Click Trash Hide the holiday.

Unhide individual holidays from your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left panel, click Add calendar Browse calendars of interest.
  • Next to your preferred holiday calendar, click Expand .
  • Select specific holidays and observances you want to show on your calendar.

Subscribe to holiday calendars published on the web

You can use a link to add many third-party holiday calendars published on the web to Google Calendar.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, next to “Other calendars,” click Add other calendars From URL.
  • Enter the URL of the published calendar.
  • Click Add calendar.
    • The calendar displays on the left, under “Other calendars.”

Change the color of holiday events

  • Open Google Calendar.

  • On the left, find the holiday calendar.

  • Click Options .

  • Select a new color. Tips:

  • The color applies to all holiday events on your calendar.

  • You can’t individually change the color of holiday events.

Related resources

  • Change Google Calendar settings
  • Use labels to track calendar entries

Add or remove holidays on your calendar (iPhone and iPad)

Important: To learn more about changes on how Calendar shows holidays and other moments, check The Keyword blog.

Holidays are automatically added to your calendar. You can update your calendar settings to:

  • Show public holidays and other observances, public holidays only, or selected holidays.
  • Add or remove holidays from other countries or regions.
  • Add or remove religious holidays.
  • Hide or unhide individual holidays.
  • Change the color of holiday events. To show the holidays most important to you, you can customize your calendar.

Tips:

  • Calendar shows public holidays and national observances using information from a third-party company that specializes in this data. This information is regularly updated and available for every country where Calendar can be used.
  • To add specific holidays, you can subscribe to third-party holiday calendars published on the web.

Select holidays to show on your calendar

Important: You can’t delete or edit the details of individual holidays on your calendar.

Public holidays and other observances appear on your calendar by default. To show public holidays only:

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings Holidays.
  • Tap National Holidays.
  • Select your preferred country or region.
  • Under the country or region you’ve selected, select one of these:
    • All holidays
    • Public holidays only

Add or remove country and region holidays

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings Holidays.
  • Tap the country or region you’re subscribed to.
    • To add, select a country from the list All holidays or Public holidays only.
    • To remove, select a country from the list Don’t show.

Add or remove religious holidays

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings Holidays.
  • Tap Add religious holidays.
  • To add or remove a religious holiday, tap it.

Hide all holidays from your calendar

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu .
  • To the left of “Holidays,” tap the box. Tip: Calendars with an empty box are already hidden from your calendar.

Hide individual holidays from your calendar

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • Select a holiday.
  • Tap More options Hide this holiday Hide.

Unhide individual holidays from your calendar

Important: You can unhide all hidden holidays on your phone, but for individual holidays, you must use your computer.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings Holidays.
  • To restore all individually hidden holidays, tap your preferred holiday calendar All holidays or Public holidays only.

Change the color of holiday events

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .

  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings Holidays.

  • Select a new color. Tips:

  • The color applies to all holiday events on your calendar.

  • You can’t individually change the color of holiday events.

Related resources

  • Change Google Calendar settings
  • Use labels to track calendar entries

Create & manage a public Google calendar

You can share your calendar publicly so anyone can check it.

How public calendars work

When you make a calendar public, other people can:

  • Find your public calendar on a website. If this website is searchable, people can find your calendar in search results.
  • Sync your calendar with other applications.
  • Subscribe to your public calendar and check it in Google Calendar. When you make a calendar public, you can choose to let other people check all the event details or just when you’re free or busy.

Make your calendar public

You can have multiple calendars and share them with different people. For example, you can have a public calendar you share with everyone, and a private calendar that only you can check.

Important: You can only edit Google Calendar sharing settings from a computer, not the mobile app.

Mark your calendar as public

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left, under “Settings for my calendars,” click the name of the calendar you want to share.
  • Under “Access permissions for events,” check the box next to “Make available to public.”
  • Click OK. Tip: If you don’t want other people to read the details of your events, select See only free/busy (hide details).

Google Workspace users: Your domain’s administrator may restrict your sharing settings. If you can’t find some sharing settings, contact your domain administrator.

Stop sharing your calendar publicly

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “My calendars,” point to the calendar you don’t want to share publicly anymore.
  • Click Options Settings and sharing.
  • Under “Access permissions for events,” uncheck the box next to “Make available to public”. Tip: This change usually only takes a few minutes, but may take up to 4 hours to take effect.

Share your public calendar

There are a few ways to share your public calendar:

Embed your calendar on a website

You can embed a calendar on a website. Learn how to add a Google calendar to your website.

Share it with a certain person

You can share your calendar with a certain person. Learn how to share your public calendar with someone.

Share a link

You can share your calendar as a link to let others access your calendar in a web browser, Google Calendar, or another application.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left side, under “Settings for my calendars,” click the name of the calendar you want to share.
    • To allow people to subscribe to your calendar: Under “Access permissions for events,” click Get shareable link Copy link.
    • To get a link that doesn’t require people to sign in or subscribe: Click Integrate calendar copy the link in “Public URL to this calendar” to share it.
    • To share a calendar that can be accessed in iOS: Click Integrate calendar copy the link in “Public address in iCal format.” The iCal address only works if the calendar is public.

Add events to a public calendar

If you share multiple calendars with other people, you must select the correct calendar when you create a new event.

  • Unless you change the setting, new events match the calendar’s default settings. For example, if the default is set to “public,” anyone can find your new event.
  • To override your calendar’s default settings, change your event privacy settings.

Related articles

  • Add a Google calendar to your website
  • Share your calendar with someone
  • Change your event privacy settings

Create a calendar for a room or shared space

If you manage a shared space, like a conference room or basketball court, you can create a calendar for the space and let people book time in it.

Note: If you’re using Google Calendar through your work, school, or organization, learn how to add resources to a work or school event instead.

Create a shared space calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar. You can’t create shared calendars from the Google Calendar app.
  • On the left, next to “Other calendars,”click Add other calendars Create new calendar.
  • Add a name for your calendar, and any other information you want to include.
  • At the bottom of the page, click Create calendar.
  • On the top left, click Back .
  • Under “My calendars,”next to your new calendar’s name, click More Settings and sharing.
  • Under “Auto-accept invitations,” select an option:
    • Auto-accept invitations that do not conflict: All invitations show on the calendar. If there are no conflicts, people with permission to view all event details automatically accept an invite to any new event.
    • Automatically add all invitations to this calendar: All invitations show on the calendar. People can RSVP to the invitation.
    • Do not show invitations: Invitations don’t show on the calendar. People subscribed to the calendar must turn on notifications to receive invitations for new events in their email. After they RSVP in the email notification, the event shows up on the calendar.
  • Make your calendar public, or share it with certain email addresses.
  • On the top left, click Back .

Add a shared space calendar

You can add calendars created by other people in your organization, such as room schedules, to your list.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar. You can’t add shared calendars from the Google Calendar app.
  • On the left, next to “Other calendars,” click Add Browse resources.
  • Next to the calendar you want to add, check the box.
  • On the top left, click Back .
  • You’ll see the new calendar under “Other calendars.”

Options

Set Calendar hours

To set hours for your space, block off the time when it isn’t available.

  • Open the calendar you created for your space.
  • For the hours that aren’t available, create an event during that time with a name like “Closed.” If the space is always unavailable at that time, you can make the event repeat.
  • Click Save. Create an event that shows up on a shared calendar

Step 1: Get the ID of the calendar you want to share

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “My calendars,” point to the calendar you want to share.
  • Click More Settings and sharing.
  • On the left, click Integrate calendar.
  • Copy the Calendar ID.

Step 2: Create an event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top left, click Create .
  • Enter the event information.
  • Under “Add guests,” add the calendar ID.
  • Click Save.

Manage birthdays on your calendar

You can keep track of upcoming birthdays using Google Calendar. You can create, hide, or change the color of birthdays on your calendar.

  • The birthday information syncs with your calendar when you add a person and include their birthday in Google Contacts.
  • You can edit or remove birthday information about the person in the Contacts app for Android or Google Contacts.
  • You can hide all birthdays on your calendar. This hides both manually created birthdays and those imported from Google Contacts.
  • If you use Calendar on an Android device, iPhone, or iPad: You can edit birthdays that are linked to Google Contacts.
    • If you edit a birthday in Calendar, the same edits are applied in Google Contacts.
  • When you delete a birthday from Calendar, it doesn’t affect the information stored in Google Contacts.
  • If you turn off “Sync from Contacts” in calendar settings it deletes all synced events in Calendar which includes:
    • Birthdays
    • Important dates
    • Any changes made to these events Add or edit birthdays in Google Contacts

Hide all birthdays on your calendar

You can hide all birthdays on your calendar. This hides both manually created birthdays and those imported from Google Contacts.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, find “My calendars.”
    • To expand it, click My calendars.
  • Hover your mouse over “Birthdays.”
  • Uncheck the box.

Change the color of birthdays on your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, find “My calendars.”
    • To expand it, click My calendars.
  • Hover your mouse over “Birthdays.”
  • Click Options .
  • Select a new color.

Turn off birthdays from one of your accounts

If you have multiple Google Accounts, you can hide birthdays imported from Contacts associated with one of your accounts.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • Under “Settings for my Calendars,” click Birthdays.
  • Under “Sync from Contacts,” next to the Google Accounts you want to hide, uncheck the boxes. Tip: By default, sync is on for the Google Accounts on your calendar. If you uncheck any of the Google Accounts, birthdays imported from Contacts are removed from your Calendar but remain in Contacts.

  • Use color labels to track calendar entries

Create a birthday event on your calendar

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the bottom right, tap Create Event.
  • At the top, select Birthday.
  • Add a name and birth date.
    • You receive a reminder notification one week before, and on the birthday.
      • To remove an existing notification, next to it, tap Remove .
      • To add other notifications, tap Add notification.
  • At the top right, tap Save.

Hide all birthdays on your calendar

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu .
  • To the left of “Birthdays,” tap the box. Tip: Calendars with an empty box are already hidden from your calendar.

Turn off birthdays from one of your accounts

If you have multiple accounts, you can hide birthdays imported from Contacts associated with one of your accounts.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings.
  • Tap Birthdays.
  • To hide an account, turn off Sync from Contacts. Tip: By default, sync is on for the Google Accounts on your calendar. If you uncheck any of the Google Accounts, birthdays imported from Contacts are removed from your Calendar but remain in Contacts.

Change the color of birthdays on your calendar

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .

  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings.

  • Tap Birthdays.

  • Select a new color. Important:

  • The birthdays in Apple Contacts now show as Calendar events on your iPhone or iPad along with your Google Contacts. However they don’t sync to Android devices or the Web.

  • Default notification settings apply to these birthdays.

  • In the “Settings” page, under the “From Device” account label, you can change notification settings.

Use a family calendar on Google

When you create a family on Google, a calendar called “Family” is automatically created. Anyone who joins your family or has access to your family calendar can find it when they open Google Calendar on any device where they’re signed in.

Learn how the family calendar works

  • The family manager is the default owner of the family calendar but can transfer ownership to someone else. Learn how to transfer calendars or events in Google Calendar.
  • Anyone in the family or anyone with access to your family calendar can:
    • Make changes to events on the family calendar.
    • Manage how the family calendar is shared.
    • Edit the family calendar’s name.
  • On your computer, you can share the family calendar with non-family members.
  • If someone leaves a family group, they lose access to the family calendar.
  • If a family manager deletes the family group, the family calendar and related events must be deleted manually.
  • Anyone with “Make changes and manage sharing” permission gets an email notification when a user under the age of 13:
    • Shares the family calendar
    • Makes the family calendar public
    • Changes member permissions

Create an event on the family calendar

  • Open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Click Event.
  • Next to Calendars , click the drop-down and select the family calendar.
  • Add a title and event details. If you want, you can invite non-family members to join your event.
  • Click Save.

Change your family calendar notification settings

By default, you get the same notifications for upcoming events as you do for your primary calendar. However, you don’t get notifications when a family member creates, edits, or deletes an event.

To change your notification settings for upcoming events:

  • Open Google Calendar.
  • On the left side of the page, find the “My calendars” section.
  • Hover over the name of your family calendar, then click Options .
  • Click Settings.
  • Click Event notifications.
  • To create a new notification, click Add notification.
  • To remove a notification, click Remove notification .

Share your family calendar with non-family members

Important: By default, non-family members can edit events in the family calendar. The calendar can be changed in the same way you change it for regular secondary calendars. Understand permission for shared calendars.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings menu Settings.
  • Under “Settings for my calendars,” select your family calendar Calendar settings.
  • On the right, under “Shared with,” click Add people and groups.
  • Enter the name of the person or group you want to share your calendar with.

Delete the family calendar

Important: Only the calendar owner can delete the family calendar.

If you don’t need the family calendar anymore, you can permanently delete or unsubscribe from a calendar.

Related resource

  • Manage your family on Google

To change your notification settings for upcoming events:

  • Open the Google Calendar app .
  • In the top left, tap Menu Settings .
  • Tap the name of your family calendar.
  • Tap your current notifications to remove or change them.

Delete the family calendar

Create events in Calendar or Gmail

Chat with guests from a Google Calendar event

Important: This feature is not available for personal Google Accounts (@gmail.com).

If you have a work or school account, you can chat with guests from a Google Calendar event.

When you chat with guests from an event, it opens a group conversation in Google Chat. This conversation:

  • Includes all event guests in your organization, regardless of their RSVP status.

  • Can have up to 200 guests. This also includes Google Groups in your organization, and their privacy settings are respected. If you have a work account, the conversation:

  • Sets the event organizer as the conversation owner.

  • Matches the Calendar event title and date, and syncs in real time with changes that you make to the event. If there are external Google Accounts, guests on other platforms like Outlook, and guests outside your organization on the event, they do not join the conversation.

Chat with guests

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an event.
  • Next to the number of guests, click Chat with guests .

Turn chat messaging on or off before an event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open an event.
  • At the top right, click Edit event .
  • Next to the Meet video link, click Video call options .
  • In the “Chat moderation” section, turn “Let contributors send messages” on or off. Tip: If you turn chat messages off in recurring meetings or meetings that use the same meeting code, the setting is saved for the next scheduled meeting.

How a conversation is named

  • The conversation title matches the Calendar event title and date (for example, “Sales Team Weekly - Nov 8”).
  • The date and language format in the title is based on the event organizer’s language and timezone settings.
  • The conversation appears in Google Chat, in the “Direct messages” section.

How your conversation syncs with the event

Any changes you make before the event starts automatically syncs with the conversation. For example:

  • When you update the event title, it updates the name of the conversation.
  • When you add or remove guests from the event, it also adds or removes them from the conversation.
  • If you add or remove someone in Google Chat, the change is overridden by changes in the Calendar event.
    • For example, if you add someone to the conversation, they are removed if you remove them from the corresponding event. In addition:
  • The “Chat with guests” button on the Calendar event remains as a link for eligible guests to find the chat history.
  • If a guest is removed from the chat they lose access to meeting conversation.
  • If a removed guest joins the meeting, they will only have access to messages during the meeting, and messages disappear when the meeting ends.
  • To restore full access, you must manually add them back to the chat.

Turn on “See guest list” for people invited to the Calendar event

As the event organizer, you control if people invited to your event can view the guest list. When you turn off “See guest list,” it affects how your conversation works.

If “See guest list” is:

  • Off before the meeting conversation is created:
    • The “Chat with guests” button is not available in the Calendar event for anyone.
    • Guests can only chat during the meeting. They do not get a meeting conversation in Chat, and all messages disappear when the meeting ends.
  • Off after the meeting conversation is created:
    • The “Chat with guests” button is not available in the Calendar event for anyone.
    • The chat conversation continues to exist in Google Chat for its current members.
    • However, the automatic sync between the Calendar guest list and the chat membership stops permanently.
    • Future changes to the Calendar guest list no longer update the chat membership.
  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open an event.
  • At the top right, click Edit event .
  • On the right, click the Guests tab.
  • Under “Guest permissions,” turn on See guest list.

Chat with guests from a Google Calendar event (Computer)

Important: This feature is not available for personal Google Accounts (@gmail.com).

If you have a work or school account, you can chat with guests from a Google Calendar event.

When you chat with guests from an event, it opens a group conversation in Google Chat. This conversation:

  • Includes all event guests in your organization, regardless of their RSVP status.

  • Can have up to 200 guests. This also includes Google Groups in your organization, and their privacy settings are respected. If you have a work account, the conversation:

  • Sets the event organizer as the conversation owner.

  • Matches the Calendar event title and date, and syncs in real time with changes that you make to the event. If there are external Google Accounts, guests on other platforms like Outlook, and guests outside your organization on the event, they do not join the conversation.

Chat with guests

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an event.
  • Next to the number of guests, click Chat with guests .

Turn chat messaging on or off before an event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open an event.
  • At the top right, click Edit event .
  • Next to the Meet video link, click Video call options .
  • In the “Chat moderation” section, turn “Let contributors send messages” on or off. Tip: If you turn chat messages off in recurring meetings or meetings that use the same meeting code, the setting is saved for the next scheduled meeting.

How a conversation is named

  • The conversation title matches the Calendar event title and date (for example, “Sales Team Weekly - Nov 8”).
  • The date and language format in the title is based on the event organizer’s language and timezone settings.
  • The conversation appears in Google Chat, in the “Direct messages” section.

How your conversation syncs with the event

Any changes you make before the event starts automatically syncs with the conversation. For example:

  • When you update the event title, it updates the name of the conversation.
  • When you add or remove guests from the event, it also adds or removes them from the conversation.
  • If you add or remove someone in Google Chat, the change is overridden by changes in the Calendar event.
    • For example, if you add someone to the conversation, they are removed if you remove them from the corresponding event. In addition:
  • The “Chat with guests” button on the Calendar event remains as a link for eligible guests to find the chat history.
  • If a guest is removed from the chat they lose access to meeting conversation.
  • If a removed guest joins the meeting, they will only have access to messages during the meeting, and messages disappear when the meeting ends.
  • To restore full access, you must manually add them back to the chat.

Turn on “See guest list” for people invited to the Calendar event

As the event organizer, you control if people invited to your event can view the guest list. When you turn off “See guest list,” it affects how your conversation works.

If “See guest list” is:

  • Off before the meeting conversation is created:
    • The “Chat with guests” button is not available in the Calendar event for anyone.
    • Guests can only chat during the meeting. They do not get a meeting conversation in Chat, and all messages disappear when the meeting ends.
  • Off after the meeting conversation is created:
    • The “Chat with guests” button is not available in the Calendar event for anyone.
    • The chat conversation continues to exist in Google Chat for its current members.
    • However, the automatic sync between the Calendar guest list and the chat membership stops permanently.
    • Future changes to the Calendar guest list no longer update the chat membership.
  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open an event.
  • At the top right, click Edit event .
  • On the right, click the Guests tab.
  • Under “Guest permissions,” turn on See guest list.

  • Tap Guests can.
  • Turn on See guest list.

Create a recurring event

When you create an event in Google Calendar, you can make it repeat on certain days.

Set up a new repeating event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top left, click Create .
  • Add the event title and any other details.
  • Next to “Does not repeat,” click the Down arrow .
  • Choose how often you want the event to repeat, and when you want the event that repeats to end.
  • At the top right, click Save. Tip: Repeating events in Google Calendar have a limit of 730 occurrences.

Make an existing event repeat

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an event Edit .
  • Next to “Does not repeat,” click the Down arrow .
  • Choose how often you want the event to repeat, and when you want the event that repeats to end.
  • In the top right, click Save.

Make changes to events that repeat

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an event Edit .
  • Make any changes, then at the top, click Save.
  • Choose which events in the series you want to update:
    • This event: This event will be updated, but the details for all other recurring events will stay the same.
    • This and following events: This event and all events moving forward will be updated. Any events from the past won’t be updated.
      • This option appears only when editing the second occurrence of an event or any occurrence after it.
    • All events: Every event in the series, including past and future occurrences, will be updated.

Create a recurring event (Computer)

When you create an event in Google Calendar, you can make it repeat on certain days.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Add the event title and any other details.

Create a recurring event (iPhone and iPad)

When you create an event in Google Calendar, you can make it repeat on certain days.

Set up a new repeating event

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • In the bottom right corner, tap Create Event.
  • Add a title to your event and tap Done.
  • Choose the event’s date and time.
  • Under the time, tap More options Does not repeat.
  • Choose how often you want the event to repeat.
  • In the top right, tap Save. Tip: Repeating events in Google Calendar have a limit of 730 occurrences.

Make an existing event repeat

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • Tap the event you want to update.
  • Tap Edit .
  • Under the time, tap More options Does not repeat.
  • Choose how often you want the event to repeat.
  • In the top right, tap Save.

Make changes to repeating events

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • Tap the event you want to update.
  • Tap Edit .
  • Make updates to your event.
  • In the top right, tap Save.
  • Choose which events in the series you want to update:
    • This event: This event will be updated, but the details for all other recurring events will stay the same.
    • This and following events: This event and all events moving forward will be updated. Any events from the past won’t be updated.
      • This option appears only when editing the second occurrence of an event or any occurrence after it.
    • All events: Every event in the series, including past and future occurrences, will be updated.

Create an event from a Gmail message

When you create an event from a Gmail message, it opens a new tab in your browser to Google Calendar. The new event:

  • Copies the Gmail subject and message text
  • Adds the recipient on the Gmail message as an invitee
  • On your computer, go to Gmail.
  • Open a message.
  • At the top, above the message, click More Create event.
  • In your Calendar, confirm the event details.
    • To invite more people, add their email addresses.
  • Once done, click Save.

Create an event in Google Calendar

To schedule time to meet with one or more people, you can create an event in Google Calendar

Ways to create an event

Create an event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top left, click Create Event,
    • Or, click an empty time slot on your calendar grid.
  • Add a title to the event.
  • Add any event details, such as the date, time, guests, or location.
  • At the bottom right, click Save. Create an event on a shared calendar

Important: To create events in a shared calendar, you can:

  • Create a new calendar and share it. Learn how to create a new calendar.

  • Share an existing calendar. Learn how to share your calendar with someone.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • At the top left, click Create Event.

  • Add a title to the event.

  • Add any event details, such as the date, time, guests, or location.

  • At the bottom, next to the calendar name , click the Down arrow .

  • Select the shared calendar.

    • You can’t add events on Holiday or Birthday calendars.

  • At the bottom right, click Save. Tips:

  • To quickly create an event:

    • On your keyboard, press Shift + C / ⌘ + C.
    • Add your event details, then click Save.

  • To check guest availability before you create an event, on the left, click Search for people.

  • To create an all-day event, under the date and time, check the box next to All day.

Edit an event that you create

Update your event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • On your calendar grid, open an event.

  • At the top right, click Edit event .

  • Make changes to your event.

  • At the top right, click Save. Choose what guests can do with your event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Click an event Edit event .

  • At the right, under “Guest permissions,” select:

    • Modify event: Guests can add rooms, adjust the time, and make other changes to your event.
    • Invite others: Guests can invite others to your event.
    • See guest list: Guests can check which other guests are on the event list.

  • Click Save. Tips:

  • If Modify event is selected, Invite others and See guest list are automatically enabled and cannot be turned off.

  • If Modify event is not selected, you can enable or disable those permissions independently. Change the color of an event

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Click an event Edit event .

  • Next to the calendar name , click the current color.

  • Select a new color from the palette picker. Tip: Nobody else can see the color you set for your events unless they have permission to make changes to your calendar. Learn how to manage your calendar permissions.

Fix issues when you can’t create an event

If you can’t create an event, or Google Calendar doesn’t load, try these troubleshooting steps:

  • Turn on JavaScript: JavaScript is required for many features to work. Learn how to enable JavaScript.
  • Create an event from your calendar grid: Click an empty time slot on your calendar, enter a title in the pop-up box, and then click Save.
    • If you try to add an event to a shared calendar, make sure the owner gives you Make changes to events permission.
    • Without edit permission, the calendar won’t appear in your calendar list when you create an event.
  • Open Google Calendar in a private window:
    • Browser extensions can block the event edit page from loading.
    • If you can create an event in a private, or incognito, window, turn off your browser extensions one by one to find the cause.
    • Learn how to browse privately in:
      • Chrome
      • Edge
      • Firefox
      • Safari
  • Clear your browser’s cache and cookies: Learn how to clear cache and cookies.
  • Check third-party sync apps or network connections:
    • If you use a third-party app to sync your calendar, turn off sync and try again.
    • Weak internet connections or network firewalls can also prevent events from saving.
  • Open your browser and sign in to Google Calendar again: Make sure to use “https” in the web address instead of “http.”

  • Change Google Calendar notifications
  • Create an event from a Gmail message

Create an event in Google Calendar (iPhone and iPad)

To schedule time to meet with one or more people, you can create an event in Google Calendar

Ways to create an event

Create an event

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the bottom right, tap Create Event.
    • Or, tap an empty time slot on your calendar grid.
  • Add a title to the event.
  • Add any event details, such as the date, time, guests, or location.
  • At the top right, tap Save. Create an event on a shared calendar

Important: To create events on a shared calendar:

  • On a computer, create a new calendar and share it. Learn how to create a new calendar.

  • On a computer, share an existing calendar. Learn how to share your calendar with someone.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .

  • At the bottom right, tap Create Event.

  • Add a title to the event.

  • Add any event details, such as the date, time, guests, or location.

  • Below the date and time, swipe through the carousel and choose a shared calendar.

    • You can’t add events on Holiday or Birthday calendars.

  • At the top right, tap Save. Tips:

  • To create an all-day event, above the date and time, turn on All-day.

  • Under “Guests can,” turn on permissions to let guests edit the event, invite others, or view the guest list.

  • To find a time guests are available, swipe down or tap View schedules.

  • When you switch categories in the middle of the creation process, the Calendar app retains as much of the data as possible.

Edit an event that you create

Edit an event

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • Open the event you want to edit.
  • At the top right, tap Edit event .
  • Make changes to your event.
  • At the top right, tap Save. Tip: To move an event, drag and drop it to a new time and date on your calendar.

Choose what guests can do with your event

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .

  • Open the event you want to let guests edit.

  • At the top right, tap Edit event .

  • In the “Guests can” section, turn on:

    • Modify event: Guests can add rooms, adjust the time, and make other changes to your event.
    • Invite others: Guests can invite others to your event.
    • See guest list: Guests can check which other guests are on the event list.

  • At the top right, tap Save. Tips:

  • If Modify event is selected, Invite others and See guest list are automatically enabled and cannot be turned off.

  • If Modify event is not selected, you can enable or disable those permissions independently. Change the color of an event

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .

  • Open the event you want to edit.

  • At the top right, tap Edit event .

  • Tap the current color.

  • Select a new color. Tip: Nobody else can see the color you set for your events unless they have permission to make changes to your calendar. Learn how to manage your calendar permissions.

Fix issues when you can’t create an event

If nothing happens when you tap Create , or you get an error when you tap Save, follow these steps:

  • Check which calendar is selected:
    • On the event creation screen, check the selected calendar under your account.
    • You can’t create events on holiday or view-only shared calendars.
  • Update the Google Calendar app: Learn how to manually update apps from the App Store.
  • Force close and reopen the Calendar app:
    • Swipe up from the bottom of your screen.
    • Swipe up on the Google Calendar app preview to close it, and reopen the app.
  • Check your network, VPN, or DNS settings: If you get an “An error has occurred” message when saving an event, turn off your VPN and custom DNS settings, and try again.
  • Remove and re-add your Google Account:
    • In the Calendar app, tap your profile photo Manage accounts on this device Remove from this device.
    • Restart your iPhone or iPad, then add your account back.
  • Check for sync errors: Learn how to fix sync problems with the Google Calendar app.
