Accessibility in Google Calendar (iPhone and iPad)

You can access Google Calendar on any device. It’s part of the Google Workspace suite of productivity tools, like Gmail, Google Meet, Google Docs, and more.

You can use Calendar to:

  • Create and manage events
  • Set reminders
  • Share your Calendar with others You can use Calendar with platform accessibility features and tools and market leading accessibility technologies such as screen readers. There are several built-in accessibility features in Calendar, like keyboard shortcuts and multiple view options.

For the best experience, use the latest version of your:

  • Browser
  • Operating system
  • Assistive technology Some accessibility features discussed in this article require specific local setup or configuration and might not be available in all locales.

Vision

To interact with your device with touch and spoken feedback, you can turn on VoiceOver. As you navigate and perform actions, VoiceOver describes Calendar elements and content. It also informs you about alerts and notifications.

To learn how to customize the appearance of Calendar, go to:

  • Use Dark theme in Calendar

  • View your day, week, or month

  • Use color labels to track Calendar entries If you need to adjust colors, use iOS features like:

  • Dark Mode

  • Other color adjustments You can also use these low vision assistance features:

  • Screen magnification

  • Text scaling

  • Screen resolution

  • Select to speak For more info on low vision assistance on iOS, go to Accessibility features for vision.

Hearing

Google Calendar can generally be used without hearing, but you should modify notifications to ensure that reminders are delivered in a way you can perceive. For additional notification options, go to related resources.

Alternative input

To minimize your interactions, enable these features in Calendar settings:

  • Options to manage invitations in Calendar, which includes automatically adding invitations. Learn how to manage invitations in Calendar.

  • Join scheduled meetings.

  • Get directions to event locations. These features for interaction and dexterity may be useful:

  • To navigate and interact with the Calendar user interface, set up and turn on Switch Control.

  • To navigate without touch, use Eye Tracking.

  • To navigate and interact by voice, use Voice Control.

  • Add a touchpad and cursor to the “Assistant” menu.

  • Configure:

    • AssistiveTouch
    • Touch sensitivity
    • Duration settings

  • Add a physical keyboard with:

    • Sticky keys
    • Slow keys
    • Repeat keys For more information on platform settings for interaction and dexterity, go to Accessibility features for mobility.

Cognition

Users with cognitive challenges like processing orders or distraction issues have different requirements, some need extensive hints and attention-grabbing UI to stay on task, while others need minimal distractions.

To meet these requirements, Calendar provides settings and features with toggles to turn individual features on or off. Learn how to change Google Calendar settings.

You can also go to:

  • View your day, week, or month
  • Use color labels to track Calendar entries
  • Modify Google Calendar notifications Tip: To automatically add trip itineraries and reservations to your Calendar, learn how to manage your events from Gmail.

Refer to cognitive accessibility features to explore additional features that might be useful, like:

  • Toggling animation
  • Notification timeout and appearance
  • Select to speak

Create a new calendar

To keep track of different types of events, you can create new calendars. For example, to track upcoming practices and games, create a calendar called “Football.”

Set up a new calendar

Important: You can only create new calendars from a browser. After you create a calendar, you can find it on your browser and in the Calendar app.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • On the left, next to “Other calendars,” click Add other calendars Create new calendar.

  • Add a name and description for your calendar.

  • Click Create calendar. Tips:

  • After you create and share a calendar, you can schedule events for that calendar. Learn how to create an event in a shared calendar.

  • When you create a calendar, you automatically become its owner.

    • Only the owner can permanently delete the calendar or transfer ownership to someone else. Learn how to transfer calendars or events in Google Calendar. Create a new calendar

Hide a calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “My calendars” or “Other calendars,” find the calendar you want to hide.
  • Next to the calendar’s name, click Options Hide from list.

Show a hidden calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings menu Settings.
  • On the left, scroll to the “Settings for other calendars” section.
  • To the right of the hidden calendar, click Show in calendar list .

Edit your calendar’s name

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left side of the page, under “My calendars,” find your calendar.
  • Next to your calendar, click Options Settings and sharing.
  • In the “Name” box at the top, enter a new name.

Change your calendar’s color

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left side of the page, under “My calendars,” find your calendar.
  • Next to your calendar, click Options
  • Pick the color for your calendar or click Add custom color .

Related resources

  • Share your calendar with someone
  • Create & manage a public Google calendar
  • Delete or unsubscribe from a calendar

Fix Calendar events for Canadian daylight saving time

Several Canadian provinces and territories recently decided to stay on daylight saving time permanently. As a result, some Google Calendar events created in certain time zones in Canada may appear one hour late on and after November 1, 2026. This article explains which events are affected by the change and how to update them.

Learn which Google Calendar events are affected

Your event may be one hour late if its time zone is in:

  • Alberta
  • British Columbia
  • Manitoba
  • The Northwest Territories Affected events have a warning badge on your calendar grid. When you open an event you organized, you get a banner that allows you to move the event one hour earlier.

Important:

  • Corresponding US time zones are not flagged for updates, even if you have used them interchangeably with Canadian time zones in the past.
  • To ensure your schedule is accurate, check that all your meetings are using a Canadian time zone.

Learn how Google Calendar fixes your events

In October 2026, Google Calendar will automatically shift most affected events back by one hour to their original scheduled local times. This automatic fix applies to:

  • Single events
  • Recurring events that don’t have future exceptions, such as a single rescheduled instance, a different room, or a custom description After the events move to their original times, your guests will need to RSVP to your event again. To track these time zone corrections across the calendars you manage, you can enable email notifications for changed events. Learn how to change Google Calendar notifications.

Google Calendar won’t be able to update all affected events. If you already corrected an event’s time, it won’t be moved again by Google.

Fix individual events on your calendar

Important:

  • In-product banners and one-click fixes only appear on the web version of Calendar. They don’t appear in the Calendar app for Android, iPhone, and iPad or third-party calendar apps.
  • To change the event time, you must be the organizer or have at least “Make changes (see private events as free/busy)” access to the organizer’s calendar.
  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Open an affected event.
  • To move the event, click Move one hour earlier.
    • Or, to dismiss the warning, click Keep current time. Open Calendar on web

Tips:

  • After you fix the event on the web, discrepancies can still appear in Microsoft Exchange and other third-party apps.
  • For the Google Calendar app :
    • Make sure the app is on its latest version.
    • Check that your mobile operating system is on its latest version.
    • If you still notice discrepancies between the app and Google Calendar on the web, temporarily change your calendar to a matching Greenwich Mean Time zone. Learn how to use Google Calendar in different time zones.

Fix recurring events on your calendar

Important: Custom changes to recurring events, like attachments or different guests, can be lost. This happens if you apply time changes to All events or This and following events. To avoid this issue, follow these steps.

Step 1: Update the time for an upcoming event
  • On your calendar, open the next event in the series scheduled after November 1, 2026.
  • At the top of the event, click Move one hour earlier.
  • When prompted, select This and following events.
Step 2: Recreate any custom changes for an upcoming event
  • On your calendar, open a future event in the series you want to apply a custom change to.
  • Add the custom change, such as a different guest or an attachment.
  • Click Save. Tip: After you update an event, check that it has a conference room and attendees have no scheduling conflicts for the new time.

Manage meetings from others or on secondary calendars

If you are an invited guest to a meeting, or have a shared calendar, how you should handle the time change depends on the organizer:

  • Meetings organized by a Google Calendar organizer:
    • If you are an invited guest, the organizer is responsible for the meeting.
    • You will not find in-product tools to directly fix the event. Google’s automated tools address only events owned by the organizer.
    • If you have concerns about the displayed time, reach out to the organizer.
  • Meetings organized by a Microsoft Outlook organizer:
    • An Outlook organizer might get the correct time. However, your Google Calendar version might appear incorrect due to system differences.
    • For any meeting from an Outlook organizer, whether it’s an individual event or a recurring series, you should always check with the organizer.
    • If the time is incorrect, ask the organizer to resend the invitation with the updated time. The organizer should not forward the event, as this does not fix the time.
  • Meetings on secondary calendars:
    • You only get a warning for secondary calendar events if you have at least “Make changes (see private events as free/busy)” access to that calendar.

Manage room bookings

As a result of these changes, room conflicts are likely to occur when your events shift. For example:

  • In May 2026, Team A booked a room for 9:00 AM on November 4, 2026 before time zone updates. The booking currently displays 10:00 AM.
  • Team B then booked the same room for 9:00 AM using updated time zone definitions. If you move Team A’s event back to 9:00 AM, it could cause the room to decline the shifted event. When you update an event’s time, the booked room is released, and Google Calendar attempts to rebook it automatically for the new time. If you organized the event, you get an email when a room declines an event. After you update the time, check that your conference room is still accepted and available. Learn more about why a room might decline an event.

Related resources

  • Use Google Calendar in different time zones
  • Learn why a Google Calendar meeting room declines an event

Get started with Google Calendar (Computer)

With Google Calendar, you can:

  • Schedule meetings and events.
  • Assign tasks.
  • Share your schedule.
  • Create multiple calendars.

Learn about supported browsers for Calendar

Important: There is no standalone desktop app for Google Calendar. Instead, use a supported web browser to open Google Calendar.

To download a supported browser, select it from the list:

  • Google Chrome
  • Firefox
  • Safari
  • Microsoft Edge Tip: For the best experience, update your browser to the latest version and turn on JavaScript and cookies.

Open Calendar in your browser

  • From a web browser, visit calendar.google.com.
  • Sign in to your Google Account.
  • To change your settings, at the top right corner, click Settings menu . Open Google Calendar

Tip: While you can’t download and install Calendar on your computer, you can use it offline. Learn how to use Calendar offline.

Related resources

  • Learn about supported browsers for Google Calendar
  • Change Google Calendar settings
  • Share your calendar
  • Create an event

Get started with Google Calendar (Get started)

With Google Calendar, you can:

  • Schedule meetings and events.
  • Assign tasks.
  • Share your schedule.
  • Create multiple calendars.

  • Google Chrome
  • Firefox
  • Safari
  • Microsoft Edge Tip: For the best experience, update your browser to the latest version and turn on JavaScript and cookies.

Open Calendar in your browser

  • From a web browser, visit calendar.google.com.
  • Sign in to your Google Account.
  • To change your settings, at the top right corner, click Settings menu . Open Google Calendar

Tip: While you can’t download and install Calendar on your computer, you can use it offline. Learn how to use Calendar offline.

Related resources

  • Learn about supported browsers for Google Calendar
  • Change Google Calendar settings
  • Share your calendar
  • Create an event

Get started with Google Calendar (iPhone and iPad)

With Google Calendar, you can:

  • Schedule meetings and events.
  • Assign tasks.
  • Share your schedule.
  • Create multiple calendars.

Get Google Calendar

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the App Store.
  • Find the Calendar app .
  • Tap Get.
  • Open the app and sign in to your Google Account. Tip: To use Calendar in your mobile web browser, visit calendar.google.com.

Related resources

  • Learn about supported browsers for Google Calendar
  • Change Google Calendar settings
  • Share your calendar
  • Create an event

Learn about supported browsers for Google Calendar

Important: If you’re redirected to this help page from calendar.google.com, download a supported browser or the Calendar app for Android, iPhone, or iPad.

When you open Google Calendar in a browser, make sure the browser is supported and up-to-date. If you have the latest version of your browser, you get the latest Calendar features, and improved security and performance.

Download a supported browser

Important: To use Calendar in your browser, turn on JavaScript and cookies.

To download a supported browser, select it from the list:

  • Google Chrome

  • Firefox

  • Safari

  • Microsoft Edge If you use Calendar on an older or unsupported browser:

  • Some features might not work.

  • The application might not open. For example, on an unsupported browser, you can find your calendars but not update them.

Open Calendar in your browser

  • On your device, open a supported browser.
  • Go to calendar.google.com.
  • Sign in to your Google Account.

Learn about browser updates

Important: Offline access and accessibility tools for Calendar aren’t supported in Firefox, Safari, and Microsoft Edge.

  • Google Chrome
    • You can use Calendar with the latest version of Chrome.
    • Usually, Chrome updates automatically when a new version is out.
    • If you don’t have the latest version, update Chrome.
  • Firefox, Safari, and Microsoft Edge
    • You can use Calendar with the current and previous versions of Firefox, Safari, and Microsoft Edge.
    • With each new browser update, Calendar supports the latest version and stops support for the third most recent version.

Use Calendar in a mobile browser

On most phones and tablets, you can use Calendar in a mobile browser. If you get an error:

  • On your phone, download a different browser.
  • On your browser, make sure to turn on cookies.
  • Install the Calendar app instead.

Related resources

  • Get started with Google Calendar
  • Use Google Calendar in a mobile browser

Print your calendar

In Google Calendar, you can print your calendar for a specific date range.

Customize & print your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • To select a date range, at the top right, click:
    • Day
    • Week
    • Month
    • Schedule
    • 4 days
  • At the top right, click Settings menu Print.
  • On the “Print preview” page, change details such as font size or orientation.
  • Click Print. Tip: When space is available, the locations or rooms of events are also displayed.

Set up advanced print options

Hide your calendars

On the left, under “My calendars” or “Other calendars,” click the name of the calendars you want to hide. If a calendar does not have a check mark next to it, it’s hidden.

Tip: To show a calendar, click the box.

Fit your calendar on a page

Change the orientation
  • At the top right, click Settings menu Print.
  • On the “Print preview” page, in the “Orientation” dropdown, click the Down arrow .
  • Click Landscape or Portrait.
  • Click Print.
Hide weekends
  • At the top right, click Settings menu Print.
  • On the “Print preview” page, deselect Show weekends.
  • Click Print.
Change the font size
  • At the top right, click Settings menu Print.
  • On the “Print preview” page, in the “Font size” dropdown, click the Down arrow .
  • Choose a font size.
  • Click Print. Tip: If you change the font size, it won’t change the number of days on a page. It may make your calendar easier to read.

Save your calendar as a PDF

  • Follow the steps to print your calendar.
  • In the final window with print options, under “Destination,” click Change.
  • Under “Print destinations,” click Save as PDF.
  • Click Save. Tip: To find your calendar PDF check your computer’s downloads.

Print your calendar in full color

  • At the top right, click Settings menu Print.

  • On the “Print preview” page, in the “Color & style” dropdown, click the Down arrow .

  • Click Full color.

  • Click Print. Tip: If “Full color” is selected, event responses appear as a:

  • Did not respond: Transparent box

  • No: Transparent box with crossed-out circle

  • Yes: Full color box

  • Maybe: Full color box with a question mark Include calendar event details

  • At the top right, click the “View switcher” dropdown menu Schedule.

  • At the top right, click Settings menu Print.

  • On the “Print preview” page, select:

    • Print descriptions
    • Print your response

  • Click Print.

Related resources

  • View your day, week, or month
  • Search on Google Calendar

Search on Google Calendar

You can search your calendar to find past and future events.

How to search

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • On the top right, select Search .

  • Enter your search terms.

  • Results appear as you enter text, including ones from other Google products you use, like Gmail and Google Drive.

  • Click on a result to see the details for that event. Tips:

  • To improve the search suggestions, turn on Web & App Activity. It saves your Chrome searches and browsing activity in My Activity.

  • Search results are limited to 200 events.

Filter your results

To narrow down your results, in the right of the search box, click the Down arrow .

From here, you can choose:

  • Which calendars to search
  • What (information from the event, including its name)
  • Who (names of invitees, or event owners)
  • Where (location for your event)
  • Doesn’t have
  • Date range

I don’t see all past events in my search results

If you don’t see past events when you search, try using advanced search on your computer to only see results for a certain time period.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the search box, click the Down arrow .
  • Add in information you want to filter by, including the dates.
  • At the bottom of the box, click Search.

Search on Google Calendar (Computer)

You can search your calendar to find past and future events.

How to search

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • On the top right, select Search .

  • Enter your search terms.

  • Results appear as you enter text, including ones from other Google products you use, like Gmail and Google Drive.

  • Click on a result to see the details for that event. Tips:

  • To improve the search suggestions, turn on Web & App Activity. It saves your Chrome searches and browsing activity in My Activity.

  • Search results are limited to 200 events.

Filter your results

To narrow down your results, in the right of the search box, click the Down arrow .

From here, you can choose:

  • Which calendars to search
  • What (information from the event, including its name)
  • Who (names of invitees, or event owners)
  • Where (location for your event)
  • Doesn’t have
  • Date range

If you don’t see past events when you search, try using advanced search on your computer to only see results for a certain time period.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the search box, click the Down arrow .
  • Add in information you want to filter by, including the dates.
  • At the bottom of the box, click Search.

Search on Google Calendar (iPhone and iPad)

You can search your calendar to find past and future events.

How to search

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • In the top right, tap Search .
  • Enter your search terms.
  • On the bottom right, tap Search . Tip: To improve the search suggestions, your Calendar searches and browsing activity are saved in My Activity if Web & App Activity is turned on.

I don’t see past events

If you don’t see past events when you search, try using advanced search on your computer to only see results for a certain time period.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the search box, click the Down arrow .
  • Add in information you want to filter by, including the dates.
  • At the bottom of the box, click Search.

Use Google Calendar in a mobile browser

You can use Google Calendar in your mobile browser.

Open Calendar in a mobile browser

Important: If Calendar does not open in your browser, try another browser or download the Calendar app. Learn about supported browsers for Calendar.

  • On your phone or tablet, open your browser.

  • Go to calendar.google.com.

  • Sign in to your Google Account. Tips:

  • When you’re offline, you can view events synced with Calendar. To create or edit events in a mobile browser, connect to the internet.

  • After you sign in to Calendar on a mobile browser, data may be stored on your device to improve performance. From your browser’s settings, you might be able to access and clear this data. For example, you can change your website content settings on Chrome. To learn how to clear browser data, visit your browser’s help center.

Use Calendar in a mobile browser

Important: The mobile web version of Calendar does not support notifications. To get notifications about events, install the Calendar app. Learn how to install the Calendar app.

Switch between day and month view

  • To switch between day and month view, at the top of the page, tap Day or Month.

  • To return to the current date, tap Today. Create a new event

  • On your phone or tablet, open a browser.

  • Go to calendar.google.com.

  • In the top right corner, tap Create .

  • Add details about your event.

  • To invite someone to your event, under “Add a person to this meeting,” enter their email address.

  • Tap Save. Edit & delete an event

  • On your phone or tablet, open a browser.

  • Go to calendar.google.com.

  • Open the event you want to edit.

    • To delete the event, tap Delete Ok.
    • To edit the event, tap Edit.

  • After you’ve made changes, tap Save. Hide or show a calendar

  • On your phone or tablet, open a browser.

  • Go to calendar.google.com.

  • At the bottom right corner, tap Settings.

  • To show or hide a calendar, tap the box next to it.

  • Tap Save.

Troubleshoot issues with Calendar in a mobile browser

If you get an error:

  • On your phone or tablet, download a different browser.
  • In your mobile browser, turn on cookies.
  • In your device’s app store, install the Calendar app.

Related resources

  • Learn about supported browsers for Google Calendar
  • Get started with Google Calendar

Use Google Calendar offline

With offline calendars, you can access your calendar offline. This is especially useful when you have a weak internet connection.

Even without an internet connection, you can:

  • Open Google Calendar in a Chrome browser.
  • View your calendar and events from the last 4 weeks or any time in the future.
  • View events by week, day, or month.

Use Calendar offline on desktop

  • Open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left, under “General,” click Offline Turn on offline calendar.
  • Click Reload now.
    • Your calendar will start to sync for offline use. At the top right of your calendar you’ll find Sync .
    • When the sync is complete, your calendar’s status shows Ready for offline .
    • When you’re offline, your calendar’s status shows as Offline .
      • A notice appears: “It looks like you’re offline. Some actions might not work.” Important:
  • “My calendars” and your calendar’s data will sync from the last time your computer was online.
  • When you’re offline, you can’t:
    • Create or edit events
    • Email guests
    • Access tasks
  • If you clear your computer’s cached images and files, it will also clear offline support for Google Calendar. Turn on Google Calendar offline again to use Calendar offline.

Use Google Calendar offline (Computer)

With offline calendars, you can access your calendar offline. This is especially useful when you have a weak internet connection.

Even without an internet connection, you can:

  • Open Google Calendar in a Chrome browser.
  • View your calendar and events from the last 4 weeks or any time in the future.
  • View events by week, day, or month.

Use Calendar offline on desktop

  • Open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left, under “General,” click Offline Turn on offline calendar.
  • Click Reload now.
    • Your calendar will start to sync for offline use. At the top right of your calendar you’ll find Sync .
    • When the sync is complete, your calendar’s status shows Ready for offline .
    • When you’re offline, your calendar’s status shows as Offline .
      • A notice appears: “It looks like you’re offline. Some actions might not work.” Important:
  • “My calendars” and your calendar’s data will sync from the last time your computer was online.
  • When you’re offline, you can’t:
    • Create or edit events
    • Email guests
    • Access tasks
  • If you clear your computer’s cached images and files, it will also clear offline support for Google Calendar. Turn on Google Calendar offline again to use Calendar offline.

Use Google Calendar offline (iPhone and iPad)

With offline calendars, you can access your calendar offline. This is especially useful when you have a weak internet connection.

Use Calendar offline on mobile

If you use the Google Calendar app while you’re offline, you can:

  • Find events and make changes.

  • Create, edit, or respond to events.

  • Access tasks. Tips:

  • Install the Calendar app, connect to the internet, and then sign in to your Google Account. Your account syncs automatically and you can use Google Calendar offline.

  • While you’re offline, changes don’t sync with your computer or others’ calendars until you’re online again.

  • While you’re offline, you can’t email event guests.

Use Google Calendar with a screen reader

If you use a screen reader in Google Calendar, you can:

  • Create and track events

  • Send invites to others

  • Accept or reject invitations Select an option below:

  • Before you start

  • Get started

  • Complete common tasks in Google Calendar with a screen reader

    • Navigate the events area
    • Create and manage events
    • Search for an event
    • Respond to event invitations
    • Join a video meeting from a calendar event
    • Set your availability and out-of-office status
    • Open calendar settings
    • Share your calendar
    • Manage your different calendars

Before you start

Use Google Calendar as a web application

We recommend that you set up your screen reader to navigate Google Calendar as a “web application” instead of a “webpage.”

When you use Google Calendar as a web application, you can use Google Calendar keyboard shortcuts to navigate more easily. For example, you can press the c key to create an event, the Slash key to begin a calendar search, and the t key to move to today’s date. More shortcuts are described below.

Recommended browser & screen readers

Google Calendar recommends Chrome and:

  • NVDA or JAWS on Windows
  • ChromeVox on ChromeOS
  • VoiceOver on MacOS

Get started

Set up your screen reader for use with Google Calendar

Set up your screen reader to use Google Calendar as a web application.

  • JAWS: To switch off the virtual cursor, press JAWS + z.
  • NVDA: To switch to focus mode, press NVDA + Space.
  • ChromeVox: Make sure sticky mode is off. To turn off sticky mode, press the Search key twice.
  • VoiceOver: Make sure QuickNav is off. Press the Left and Right arrow keys until VoiceOver says “QuickNav Off.”
Open Google Calendar
  • Go to calendar.google.com.
  • If prompted, select or sign in to your account.
  • To open your calendar, use the “Skip to main content” button.
  • To review keyboard shortcuts, use the “Keyboard shortcuts” button.
Learn about the Google Calendar interface

In Google Calendar, there are 4 main areas:

  • Banner area: This area has buttons and links to:
    • Toggle Main drawer visibility
    • Control the events view area
    • Perform a Search query
      • Use keyboard shortcut Slash. To return to the Banner area, press Escape.
    • Get support
    • Open Settings menu
    • Open View switcher menu
    • Open other applications
    • Change the active account
  • Events area: This is the main area. It’s where events are shown in schedule, day, week, month, or year view. We recommend the use of schedule view, also called agenda view, with a screen reader.
    • Use keyboard shortcut a or the shortcut for your preferred view.
  • Main drawer area: When expanded, this area is where you can:
    • Create an Event, Out of office, Task, and more
    • Find a small calendar to the left of the main events area
    • Search for people to meet
      • Use keyboard shortcut Plus.
    • Add other calendars to the main events area
  • Right sidebar: When expanded, this area provides quick access to other Google apps, like Keep, Tasks, and Contacts that show in the side panel.
    • Windows: Press Ctrl + Alt + period.
    • ChromeOS: Press Shift + Alt + period.
    • MacOS: Press Command + Option + period.
Shortcuts in Google Calendar

You can use keyboard shortcuts to help navigate your calendar and perform tasks.

There are 2 ways to get a list of keyboard shortcuts:

  • Use keyboard shortcuts:
    • On Windows or ChromeOS, press Ctrl + Slash.
    • On MacOS, press Command + Slash.
  • Use the Tab key and buttons: Tab past the “Skip to main content” button at the top of the page to the “Keyboard shortcuts” button and press Enter.

Go to a specific date

To change the view to a specific date, use the “Go to date” shortcut:

  • To open the “Go to date” dialog, press the g key.

  • Enter the desired date. For example, 05/06/2021.

  • Press Enter. Tips:

  • When you use today’s date, the g shortcut for “Go to date” behaves the same as the t shortcut for “Go to today.”

  • In week, month, and year views, the beginning of the period that includes the new date scrolls into view, but the focus won’t move. In order to move focus to the exact new date, press the a key for schedule view or the d key for day view.

Use the small calendar view

There’s a small calendar in the Main drawer area. You can use the small calendar to change the dates in the current view or to review all days in a month.

Find the small calendar
  • In the Main drawer area, press Plus for “People search.”
  • Press Shift + Tab once to put focus on the month table. Important: If you can’t find the small calendar, it might be because the Main drawer area is collapsed. To expand the “Main drawer” button, navigate to the start of the Banner area and press Enter.
Navigate the small calendar
  • To find the desired date, use the Arrow keys. When you find the desired date, press Enter. The main calendar view updates to include the date you choose.
  • To navigate back to the main calendar area, press the a key to return to the agenda view with focus on the new date.
Create & manage events
Create an event
  • There are 3 ways to create an event:
    • Press the c key. A page opens to create a regular event.
    • Press Shift + c. A dialog opens. Choose the type of event you want to create. You can create a regular event, an out of office event, a task, or a reminder.
    • Use the “Create” menu button in the Main drawer area. You can select “Event”, “Focus time”, “Out of office”, “Task”, or “Appointment schedule.”
  • Initial focus is on an entry field for the event title.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “date and time fields,” and enter date and time info.
    • Optional: Press Tab to get to the “recurrence” fields and choose a recurrence. Learn how to create a recurring event.
    • Optional: To skip manual time and date input, use suggested times or use “find a time”.
  • Optional: To invite guests to the event, press Tab until you reach the “Guests” edit field, and then enter your guest’s email address.
    • As you enter a name or email address, a suggestion is announced. If that’s the person you want to invite, press Enter.
    • More suggestions are listed below the edit field. To listen to the names, press the Down arrow key. When the desired person is found, press Enter.
    • If a person isn’t suggested, continue to enter their name, and the suggestion list changes.
  • Optional: Populate other fields, like:
    • Rooms or meeting info
    • Notifications
    • Attachments
    • Meeting description
  • There are different ways to save an event:
    • ChromeOS or Windows: Press Ctrl + s or Ctrl + Enter.
    • MacOS: Press Command + s or Command + Enter.
    • On any operating system, you can press the Tab key until you get to the “Save” button. Then, press Enter.
  • To discard the event, press Escape.
Create a recurring event
  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To open the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Does not repeat” drop-down and then press Enter or Space. The drop-down opens.
  • Choose an option:
    • To select a preset event frequency, use the Arrow keys to go through the list of frequency options. When you hear the recurrence frequency you want your event to have, press Enter or Space.
    • To set a custom event frequency:
      • Arrow to the Custom button and then press Enter or Space. Enter the number of times the event repeats.
      • Press Tab to go to the “Repeat every” button. To move through the days of the week, press the Tab key. To choose a day, press Space.
      • Optional: You can choose the start and end date. Press Tab to go through the list and make a selection.
      • To save your recurring event set-up, press Tab to go to the “Done” button and then press Enter or Space.
Review an event
  • Navigate to an event in the calendar view.
  • Press Enter.
  • To review event fields with no shift in focus:
    • Windows: Press Alt + {number}
    • ChromeOS: Press Alt + Shift + {number}
    • MacOS: Press Option + {number}
    • The {number} selects the event field:
      • 1 Title
      • 2 Date and time
      • 3 Guests
      • 4 Rooms and location
      • 5 Description
      • 6 Attachments
      • 7 Notifications
  • To interact with fields you can change, press Tab.
    • Tip: You can’t access all info with the Tab key. To hear some info, you may have to use screen reader commands. To use screen reader commands with your screen reader:
      • JAWS: Turn on the virtual cursor.
      • NVDA: Turn on browse mode.
      • ChromeVox: Turn on sticky mode.
      • VoiceOver: Turn on QuickNav.
  • When done with event review, press Escape.
Add & remove attendees from an event
  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To edit the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • To invite guests to the event, press Tab until you reach the “Guests” edit field, and then enter your guest’s email address.
    • As you enter a name or email address, a suggestion is announced. If that’s the person you want to invite, press Enter.
    • More suggestions are listed below the edit field. To listen to the names, press the Down arrow key. When the desired person is found, press Enter.
    • If a person isn’t suggested, continue to enter their name, and the suggestion list changes.
  • Optional: You can mark a guest’s attendance as optional.
    • To mark a guest as optional, press Tab until you get to the list of invited guests.
    • Use the Arrow keys to get to the guest you want to mark as optional.
    • Press Tab to go to the “Mark optional” button and then press Enter or Space.
  • Optional: You can remove a guest.
    • To remove a guest, press Tab to get to the list of invited guests.
    • Use the Arrow keys to go to the guest you want to remove.
    • Press Tab until you get to the “Remove” button and then press Enter.
  • Save the event.
Add & remove rooms for an event

You can add and remove rooms to events if your Google Account is through your work or school.

  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To edit the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Guests” tablist. To get to the Rooms tab field, use the Right arrow key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Rooms list.”
  • To enter the list of rooms, press the Down arrow key.
  • To toggle room selection, press Enter.
  • Save the event.
Use suggested times

After you add guests, Google Calendar provides suggested times when everyone is available. To review the times and choose:

  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To edit the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Guest” edit field, and then press Enter. Add the guests to the event.
  • Press Tab to get to the “Suggested times” menu.
  • To open the menu, press Enter or the Down arrow key.
  • To review time options, use the Arrow keys.
  • When you find the time you want to choose, press Enter. The date and times in the meeting update to the suggested time.
  • Save the event.
Use “find a time”
  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To open the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Guest” edit field, and then press Enter. Add the guest to the event.
  • Press Tab to get to the “Event Details” tablist. To get to the “Find a Time” tab, use the Right arrow key.
  • To enter the calendar panel, press Tab.
  • To review time options, use the Arrow keys.
  • When you find the time you want to choose, press Enter. The date and times in the meeting update to the suggested time.
  • Save the event.
Get someone else’s calendar

You can temporarily add someone’s calendar to your calendar view.

  • Press the Plus key.

  • Enter the person’s email address.

  • Press Enter.

  • The other person’s calendar is added to your calendar view. Tips:

  • To remove the person’s calendar from the main calendar area, refresh the page.

  • If you create an event, Google Calendar automatically removes the other person’s calendar from your main calendar area.

Delete an event

Important: When you delete an event, you cancel the event for all attendees. When you remove an event from only your calendar, it doesn’t cancel the event for the other attendees. To delete an event:

  • Go to an event.
  • There are several ways to delete the event.
    • Press Backspace.
    • Press Delete.
    • To open the event dialog, press Enter. Use the Arrow keys to get to the “Delete” button and then press Enter.
    • Delete from the edit event page:
      • Press e to edit the event.
      • Press Tab until you get to the “More actions” button and then press Enter.
      • Use the Down arrow key to the “Delete” button and then press Enter.
  • Optional: If the event recurs, follow the steps in remove one or all event recurrences.
Remove one or all event recurrences

If an event is part of a series, a dialog box asks if only one or all should be removed. To choose remove one or all event recurrences:

  • Use the Up or Down arrow keys and choose whether to delete:
    • This event
    • This and following events
    • All events
  • Optional: If other guests are invited to the meeting, you can enter a message about the cancellation. After you enter your message, press Tab.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “OK” button and then press Enter.
Restore an event

If you delete an event by mistake, you can view or restore it for 30 days. To restore an event:

  • Go to the Banner area with keyboard shortcut Slash.
  • Press Escape.
  • Press Tab twice until you get to the “Settings” menu button.
  • To open the Settings menu, press Enter or the Down arrow key.
  • Use the Down arrow key until you get to the “Trash” button.
  • Press Enter.
  • Press Tab until you get to the button labeled “Restore {event name},” where {event name} matches the event you want to restore.
  • Press Space or Enter.
  • Press the browser back shortcut to return to your calendar.
Search for an event
Use simple search
  • Press the Slash key.
  • Enter a search term.
    • If you enter a contact name, press Down arrow to the matching contact.
  • Press Enter.
    • If you search multiple terms and people, you find events that match all search terms. If any events match your search, a schedule view displays with focus on the match closest to today’s date. When there are multiple matches, you can navigate like you do in the schedule view:
  • To navigate past dates with an event that matches your search, press Left arrow.
  • To navigate future dates with a matched event, press Right arrow.
  • To navigate matched events in the past, press Up arrow.
  • To navigate matched events in the future, press Down arrow.
  • To review an event, press Enter on the event. To return to the results view, press Escape.
  • To return to the previous calendar view, press Escape.
Use advanced search
  • Press the Slash key.
  • Press Tab to the “Search options” button.
  • To open a form with search options, press Enter.
  • To select an option, press Tab until you reach the option you want:
    • Which calendars to search?
      • The default is “all active.” For other options, press Down arrow.
    • What keywords must be in the event?
    • Who is a participant?
    • Where is the event?
    • What keywords must not be in the event?
    • When is the range of dates?
  • To perform the search, press Enter.
  • To navigate the results, use the Arrow keys as described above in Use simple search.
Respond to event invitations
From the events view
  • Navigate to an event.
  • Open the context menu.
    • ChromeOS: Press Shift + F10, or with ChromeVox active, press Search + m.
    • Windows: Press Shift + F10 or press the Application key.
    • MacOS with VoiceOver: Press VO + Shift + m.
  • To choose an RSVP option, press the shortcut key below or Arrow to the desired item, then press Enter. Response

Shortcut

Yes

y

Yes, in a meet room

r

Yes, joining virtually

v

No

n

Maybe

m

From the event dialog
  • To navigate to an existing event, use the Arrow keys or the Tab key. When you get to the event, press Enter.
  • Navigate to “Going?”
  • Press Tab to go to the “Yes, No, or Maybe” buttons. To select an RSVP option, press Enter.
  • Optional: If the event recurs, choose your response and then press Enter:
    • This event
    • This and following events
    • All events
From the event page
  • To navigate to an existing event, use the Arrow keys or the Tab key.
  • When you get to the event, press the e key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Going?” menu and use the Up or Down arrow keys to go to the “Yes, No, or Maybe” buttons.
  • To choose an RSVP option, press Enter.
  • Save the event.
From email
  • In your email, open the email invitation.
  • Press Tab until you get to “Going?” and use the Arrow keys or Tab key to go to the “Yes, No, or Maybe” buttons.
  • To select an RSVP option, press Enter.
Change how you’re notified for a new event

When you create an event, you can choose whether Calendar sends you updates by email or system notification.

  • Open Google Calendar.
  • To create an event, press c.
  • Fill out the event details.
  • In the “Notification method” combo box, choose how and when you want to be reminded.
    • You can pick between notification alerts or email and choose when you get the notification.
    • To add another notification method or time, click Add notification.
    • To remove a notification, click Remove notification.
  • Click Save.
Add or change notification for an existing event
  • Open Google Calendar.
  • Select an event.
  • To edit, press e.
  • In the “Notification method” combo box, choose how and when you want to be reminded.
    • You can pick between notification alerts or email and choose when you get the notification.
    • To add another notification method or time, click Add notification.
    • To remove a notification, click Remove notification.
  • Click Save.
Join a video meeting from a calendar event
  • Navigate to an event.
  • There are 2 ways to join a meeting:
    • Option 1: Use the context menu
      • To open the context menu:
        • ChromeOS: Press Shift + F10, or with ChromeVox active, press Search + m.
        • Windows: Press Shift + F10 or press the Application key.
        • MacOS with VoiceOver: Press VO + Shift + m.
      • Press j or use the Down arrow key until you get to the “Join meeting” button and then press Enter.
    • Option 2: Use the event dialog
      • Press Enter to open the event dialog.
      • Press Tab until you get to the “Join now” button and then press Enter.
Set your availability & out-of-office status

When you indicate that you’re out of the office, your calendar creates events that automatically decline all meetings during your out-of-office time.

  • To create a new out-of-office event, press Shift + c.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Event” tablist. Use the Arrow keys to get to the “Out of office” tab.
  • Press Tab until you get to the date parameters. Set the time or dates you’ll be out of office.
  • Save the event.
Open calendar settings

To get to settings, press the s key.

You can also navigate to settings:

  • Navigate to the Banner area with the Slash key.
  • Press Escape.
  • Press Tab twice to the settings menu.
  • Press Enter to open the settings menu.
  • Press Enter again to open settings.
Share your calendar
  • Press the s key to open Settings.
  • Press Shift + Tab until you get to “Settings for my calendars.”
  • Press Down arrow to find the calendar you want to share and then press Enter.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Add people” button and then press Enter or Space.
  • To share your calendar with someone, enter their email address.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Send” button and then press Enter or Space.
Manage your different calendars
Add calendars
  • Press the s key to open Settings.
  • Press Shift + Tab until you get to “General.”
  • Press Left arrow to collapse, then Down arrow to “Add Calendar,” then Enter.
  • Select an option and then press Enter:
    • Subscribe to calendar: You can subscribe to and view other calendars.
    • Create a new calendar: You can create new calendars.
    • Browse resources: This is a list of resources that are schedulable. Each resource is nested under a tabbable location.
    • Browse calendars of interest: This is a list of interest-based calendars, like sports calendars and holiday calendars.
    • From URL: You can add a new calendar from a link.
Show & hide calendars from view
  • Navigate to the Main drawer area with the Plus key.
    • If the Main drawer area isn’t available, navigate to the start of the Banner area and press Enter to expand the “Main drawer” button.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “My calendars” button.
    • If the “My calendars” button is collapsed, press Enter.
  • Press Tab to get to the first calendar.
  • To find the calendar you want, use the Down arrow key.
  • To show or hide the calendar, press Space.
  • For additional options, press Tab.
View other calendars

You can add other calendars to your calendar.

  • Navigate to the Main drawer area with the Plus key.
    • If the Main drawer area isn’t available, navigate to the start of the Banner area and press Enter to expand the “Main drawer” button.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Other calendars” button.
    • If the “Other calendars” button is collapsed, press Enter.
  • Press Tab to the “Add other calendars” button and press Enter.
  • Use the Up or Down arrow keys to select a menu option:
    • Subscribe
    • Browse
    • Create
  • Press Enter to add the calendar.
  • Press Tab to get to the first calendar.
  • To find the calendar you want, use the Down arrow key.
  • To show or hide the calendar, press Space.
  • For additional options, press Tab.
Identify which calendar an event is in

To find out who created an event or whose calendar the event is on:

  • Press e to open the event details and use virtual cursor or browse mode to navigate the dialog. The organizer is near the bottom.
  • Use arrow keys in virtual cursor off or focus mode. You’ll hear the calendar name as you highlight calendar entries.

Use Google Calendar with a screen reader (Computer)

If you use a screen reader in Google Calendar, you can:

  • Create and track events

  • Send invites to others

  • Accept or reject invitations Select an option below:

  • Before you start

  • Get started

  • Complete common tasks in Google Calendar with a screen reader

    • Navigate the events area
    • Create and manage events
    • Search for an event
    • Respond to event invitations
    • Join a video meeting from a calendar event
    • Set your availability and out-of-office status
    • Open calendar settings
    • Share your calendar
    • Manage your different calendars

Before you start

Use Google Calendar as a web application

We recommend that you set up your screen reader to navigate Google Calendar as a “web application” instead of a “webpage.”

When you use Google Calendar as a web application, you can use Google Calendar keyboard shortcuts to navigate more easily. For example, you can press the c key to create an event, the Slash key to begin a calendar search, and the t key to move to today’s date. More shortcuts are described below.

Recommended browser & screen readers

Google Calendar recommends Chrome and:

  • NVDA or JAWS on Windows
  • ChromeVox on ChromeOS
  • VoiceOver on MacOS

Get started

Set up your screen reader for use with Google Calendar

Set up your screen reader to use Google Calendar as a web application.

  • JAWS: To switch off the virtual cursor, press JAWS + z.
  • NVDA: To switch to focus mode, press NVDA + Space.
  • ChromeVox: Make sure sticky mode is off. To turn off sticky mode, press the Search key twice.
  • VoiceOver: Make sure QuickNav is off. Press the Left and Right arrow keys until VoiceOver says “QuickNav Off.”
Open Google Calendar
  • Go to calendar.google.com.
  • If prompted, select or sign in to your account.
  • To open your calendar, use the “Skip to main content” button.
  • To review keyboard shortcuts, use the “Keyboard shortcuts” button.
Learn about the Google Calendar interface

In Google Calendar, there are 4 main areas:

  • Banner area: This area has buttons and links to:
    • Toggle Main drawer visibility
    • Control the events view area
    • Perform a Search query
      • Use keyboard shortcut Slash. To return to the Banner area, press Escape.
    • Get support
    • Open Settings menu
    • Open View switcher menu
    • Open other applications
    • Change the active account
  • Events area: This is the main area. It’s where events are shown in schedule, day, week, month, or year view. We recommend the use of schedule view, also called agenda view, with a screen reader.
    • Use keyboard shortcut a or the shortcut for your preferred view.
  • Main drawer area: When expanded, this area is where you can:
    • Create an Event, Out of office, Task, and more
    • Find a small calendar to the left of the main events area
    • Search for people to meet
      • Use keyboard shortcut Plus.
    • Add other calendars to the main events area
  • Right sidebar: When expanded, this area provides quick access to other Google apps, like Keep, Tasks, and Contacts that show in the side panel.
    • Windows: Press Ctrl + Alt + period.
    • ChromeOS: Press Shift + Alt + period.
    • MacOS: Press Command + Option + period.
Shortcuts in Google Calendar

You can use keyboard shortcuts to help navigate your calendar and perform tasks.

There are 2 ways to get a list of keyboard shortcuts:

  • Use keyboard shortcuts:
    • On Windows or ChromeOS, press Ctrl + Slash.
    • On MacOS, press Command + Slash.
  • Use the Tab key and buttons: Tab past the “Skip to main content” button at the top of the page to the “Keyboard shortcuts” button and press Enter.

Go to a specific date

To change the view to a specific date, use the “Go to date” shortcut:

  • To open the “Go to date” dialog, press the g key.

  • Enter the desired date. For example, 05/06/2021.

  • Press Enter. Tips:

  • When you use today’s date, the g shortcut for “Go to date” behaves the same as the t shortcut for “Go to today.”

  • In week, month, and year views, the beginning of the period that includes the new date scrolls into view, but the focus won’t move. In order to move focus to the exact new date, press the a key for schedule view or the d key for day view.

Use the small calendar view

There’s a small calendar in the Main drawer area. You can use the small calendar to change the dates in the current view or to review all days in a month.

Find the small calendar
  • In the Main drawer area, press Plus for “People search.”
  • Press Shift + Tab once to put focus on the month table. Important: If you can’t find the small calendar, it might be because the Main drawer area is collapsed. To expand the “Main drawer” button, navigate to the start of the Banner area and press Enter.
Navigate the small calendar
  • To find the desired date, use the Arrow keys. When you find the desired date, press Enter. The main calendar view updates to include the date you choose.
  • To navigate back to the main calendar area, press the a key to return to the agenda view with focus on the new date.
Create & manage events
Create an event
  • There are 3 ways to create an event:
    • Press the c key. A page opens to create a regular event.
    • Press Shift + c. A dialog opens. Choose the type of event you want to create. You can create a regular event, an out of office event, a task, or a reminder.
    • Use the “Create” menu button in the Main drawer area. You can select “Event”, “Focus time”, “Out of office”, “Task”, or “Appointment schedule.”
  • Initial focus is on an entry field for the event title.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “date and time fields,” and enter date and time info.
    • Optional: Press Tab to get to the “recurrence” fields and choose a recurrence. Learn how to create a recurring event.
    • Optional: To skip manual time and date input, use suggested times or use “find a time”.
  • Optional: To invite guests to the event, press Tab until you reach the “Guests” edit field, and then enter your guest’s email address.
    • As you enter a name or email address, a suggestion is announced. If that’s the person you want to invite, press Enter.
    • More suggestions are listed below the edit field. To listen to the names, press the Down arrow key. When the desired person is found, press Enter.
    • If a person isn’t suggested, continue to enter their name, and the suggestion list changes.
  • Optional: Populate other fields, like:
    • Rooms or meeting info
    • Notifications
    • Attachments
    • Meeting description
  • There are different ways to save an event:
    • ChromeOS or Windows: Press Ctrl + s or Ctrl + Enter.
    • MacOS: Press Command + s or Command + Enter.
    • On any operating system, you can press the Tab key until you get to the “Save” button. Then, press Enter.
  • To discard the event, press Escape.
Create a recurring event
  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To open the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Does not repeat” drop-down and then press Enter or Space. The drop-down opens.
  • Choose an option:
    • To select a preset event frequency, use the Arrow keys to go through the list of frequency options. When you hear the recurrence frequency you want your event to have, press Enter or Space.
    • To set a custom event frequency:
      • Arrow to the Custom button and then press Enter or Space. Enter the number of times the event repeats.
      • Press Tab to go to the “Repeat every” button. To move through the days of the week, press the Tab key. To choose a day, press Space.
      • Optional: You can choose the start and end date. Press Tab to go through the list and make a selection.
      • To save your recurring event set-up, press Tab to go to the “Done” button and then press Enter or Space.
Review an event
  • Navigate to an event in the calendar view.
  • Press Enter.
  • To review event fields with no shift in focus:
    • Windows: Press Alt + {number}
    • ChromeOS: Press Alt + Shift + {number}
    • MacOS: Press Option + {number}
    • The {number} selects the event field:
      • 1 Title
      • 2 Date and time
      • 3 Guests
      • 4 Rooms and location
      • 5 Description
      • 6 Attachments
      • 7 Notifications
  • To interact with fields you can change, press Tab.
    • Tip: You can’t access all info with the Tab key. To hear some info, you may have to use screen reader commands. To use screen reader commands with your screen reader:
      • JAWS: Turn on the virtual cursor.
      • NVDA: Turn on browse mode.
      • ChromeVox: Turn on sticky mode.
      • VoiceOver: Turn on QuickNav.
  • When done with event review, press Escape.
Add & remove attendees from an event
  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To edit the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • To invite guests to the event, press Tab until you reach the “Guests” edit field, and then enter your guest’s email address.
    • As you enter a name or email address, a suggestion is announced. If that’s the person you want to invite, press Enter.
    • More suggestions are listed below the edit field. To listen to the names, press the Down arrow key. When the desired person is found, press Enter.
    • If a person isn’t suggested, continue to enter their name, and the suggestion list changes.
  • Optional: You can mark a guest’s attendance as optional.
    • To mark a guest as optional, press Tab until you get to the list of invited guests.
    • Use the Arrow keys to get to the guest you want to mark as optional.
    • Press Tab to go to the “Mark optional” button and then press Enter or Space.
  • Optional: You can remove a guest.
    • To remove a guest, press Tab to get to the list of invited guests.
    • Use the Arrow keys to go to the guest you want to remove.
    • Press Tab until you get to the “Remove” button and then press Enter.
  • Save the event.
Add & remove rooms for an event

You can add and remove rooms to events if your Google Account is through your work or school.

  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To edit the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Guests” tablist. To get to the Rooms tab field, use the Right arrow key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Rooms list.”
  • To enter the list of rooms, press the Down arrow key.
  • To toggle room selection, press Enter.
  • Save the event.
Use suggested times

After you add guests, Google Calendar provides suggested times when everyone is available. To review the times and choose:

  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To edit the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Guest” edit field, and then press Enter. Add the guests to the event.
  • Press Tab to get to the “Suggested times” menu.
  • To open the menu, press Enter or the Down arrow key.
  • To review time options, use the Arrow keys.
  • When you find the time you want to choose, press Enter. The date and times in the meeting update to the suggested time.
  • Save the event.
Use “find a time”
  • Find an event or create a new one:
    • Navigate to an event in the Calendar view. To open the event details, press the e key.
    • To create a new event, press the c key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Guest” edit field, and then press Enter. Add the guest to the event.
  • Press Tab to get to the “Event Details” tablist. To get to the “Find a Time” tab, use the Right arrow key.
  • To enter the calendar panel, press Tab.
  • To review time options, use the Arrow keys.
  • When you find the time you want to choose, press Enter. The date and times in the meeting update to the suggested time.
  • Save the event.
Get someone else’s calendar

You can temporarily add someone’s calendar to your calendar view.

  • Press the Plus key.

  • Enter the person’s email address.

  • Press Enter.

  • The other person’s calendar is added to your calendar view. Tips:

  • To remove the person’s calendar from the main calendar area, refresh the page.

  • If you create an event, Google Calendar automatically removes the other person’s calendar from your main calendar area.

Delete an event

Important: When you delete an event, you cancel the event for all attendees. When you remove an event from only your calendar, it doesn’t cancel the event for the other attendees. To delete an event:

  • Go to an event.
  • There are several ways to delete the event.
    • Press Backspace.
    • Press Delete.
    • To open the event dialog, press Enter. Use the Arrow keys to get to the “Delete” button and then press Enter.
    • Delete from the edit event page:
      • Press e to edit the event.
      • Press Tab until you get to the “More actions” button and then press Enter.
      • Use the Down arrow key to the “Delete” button and then press Enter.
  • Optional: If the event recurs, follow the steps in remove one or all event recurrences.
Remove one or all event recurrences

If an event is part of a series, a dialog box asks if only one or all should be removed. To choose remove one or all event recurrences:

  • Use the Up or Down arrow keys and choose whether to delete:
    • This event
    • This and following events
    • All events
  • Optional: If other guests are invited to the meeting, you can enter a message about the cancellation. After you enter your message, press Tab.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “OK” button and then press Enter.
Restore an event

If you delete an event by mistake, you can view or restore it for 30 days. To restore an event:

  • Go to the Banner area with keyboard shortcut Slash.
  • Press Escape.
  • Press Tab twice until you get to the “Settings” menu button.
  • To open the Settings menu, press Enter or the Down arrow key.
  • Use the Down arrow key until you get to the “Trash” button.
  • Press Enter.
  • Press Tab until you get to the button labeled “Restore {event name},” where {event name} matches the event you want to restore.
  • Press Space or Enter.
  • Press the browser back shortcut to return to your calendar.
Search for an event
Use simple search
  • Press the Slash key.
  • Enter a search term.
    • If you enter a contact name, press Down arrow to the matching contact.
  • Press Enter.
    • If you search multiple terms and people, you find events that match all search terms. If any events match your search, a schedule view displays with focus on the match closest to today’s date. When there are multiple matches, you can navigate like you do in the schedule view:
  • To navigate past dates with an event that matches your search, press Left arrow.
  • To navigate future dates with a matched event, press Right arrow.
  • To navigate matched events in the past, press Up arrow.
  • To navigate matched events in the future, press Down arrow.
  • To review an event, press Enter on the event. To return to the results view, press Escape.
  • To return to the previous calendar view, press Escape.
Use advanced search
  • Press the Slash key.
  • Press Tab to the “Search options” button.
  • To open a form with search options, press Enter.
  • To select an option, press Tab until you reach the option you want:
    • Which calendars to search?
      • The default is “all active.” For other options, press Down arrow.
    • What keywords must be in the event?
    • Who is a participant?
    • Where is the event?
    • What keywords must not be in the event?
    • When is the range of dates?
  • To perform the search, press Enter.
  • To navigate the results, use the Arrow keys as described above in Use simple search.

  • Open the context menu.
    • ChromeOS: Press Shift + F10, or with ChromeVox active, press Search + m.
    • Windows: Press Shift + F10 or press the Application key.
    • MacOS with VoiceOver: Press VO + Shift + m.

Yes, joining virtually

v

No

n

Maybe

m

From the event dialog
  • To navigate to an existing event, use the Arrow keys or the Tab key. When you get to the event, press Enter.
  • Navigate to “Going?”
  • Press Tab to go to the “Yes, No, or Maybe” buttons. To select an RSVP option, press Enter.
  • Optional: If the event recurs, choose your response and then press Enter:
    • This event
    • This and following events
    • All events
From the event page
  • To navigate to an existing event, use the Arrow keys or the Tab key.
  • When you get to the event, press the e key.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Going?” menu and use the Up or Down arrow keys to go to the “Yes, No, or Maybe” buttons.
  • To choose an RSVP option, press Enter.
  • Save the event.
From email
  • In your email, open the email invitation.
  • Press Tab until you get to “Going?” and use the Arrow keys or Tab key to go to the “Yes, No, or Maybe” buttons.
  • To select an RSVP option, press Enter.
Change how you’re notified for a new event

When you create an event, you can choose whether Calendar sends you updates by email or system notification.

  • Open Google Calendar.
  • To create an event, press c.
  • Fill out the event details.
  • In the “Notification method” combo box, choose how and when you want to be reminded.
    • You can pick between notification alerts or email and choose when you get the notification.
    • To add another notification method or time, click Add notification.
    • To remove a notification, click Remove notification.
  • Click Save.
Add or change notification for an existing event
  • Open Google Calendar.
  • Select an event.
  • To edit, press e.
  • In the “Notification method” combo box, choose how and when you want to be reminded.
    • You can pick between notification alerts or email and choose when you get the notification.
    • To add another notification method or time, click Add notification.
    • To remove a notification, click Remove notification.
  • Click Save.
Join a video meeting from a calendar event
  • Navigate to an event.
  • There are 2 ways to join a meeting:
    • Option 1: Use the context menu
      • To open the context menu:
        • ChromeOS: Press Shift + F10, or with ChromeVox active, press Search + m.
        • Windows: Press Shift + F10 or press the Application key.
        • MacOS with VoiceOver: Press VO + Shift + m.
      • Press j or use the Down arrow key until you get to the “Join meeting” button and then press Enter.
    • Option 2: Use the event dialog
      • Press Enter to open the event dialog.
      • Press Tab until you get to the “Join now” button and then press Enter.
Set your availability & out-of-office status

When you indicate that you’re out of the office, your calendar creates events that automatically decline all meetings during your out-of-office time.

  • To create a new out-of-office event, press Shift + c.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Event” tablist. Use the Arrow keys to get to the “Out of office” tab.
  • Press Tab until you get to the date parameters. Set the time or dates you’ll be out of office.
  • Save the event.
Open calendar settings

To get to settings, press the s key.

You can also navigate to settings:

  • Navigate to the Banner area with the Slash key.
  • Press Escape.
  • Press Tab twice to the settings menu.
  • Press Enter to open the settings menu.
  • Press Enter again to open settings.
Share your calendar
  • Press the s key to open Settings.
  • Press Shift + Tab until you get to “Settings for my calendars.”
  • Press Down arrow to find the calendar you want to share and then press Enter.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Add people” button and then press Enter or Space.
  • To share your calendar with someone, enter their email address.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Send” button and then press Enter or Space.
Manage your different calendars
Add calendars
  • Press the s key to open Settings.
  • Press Shift + Tab until you get to “General.”
  • Press Left arrow to collapse, then Down arrow to “Add Calendar,” then Enter.
  • Select an option and then press Enter:
    • Subscribe to calendar: You can subscribe to and view other calendars.
    • Create a new calendar: You can create new calendars.
    • Browse resources: This is a list of resources that are schedulable. Each resource is nested under a tabbable location.
    • Browse calendars of interest: This is a list of interest-based calendars, like sports calendars and holiday calendars.
    • From URL: You can add a new calendar from a link.
Show & hide calendars from view
  • Navigate to the Main drawer area with the Plus key.
    • If the Main drawer area isn’t available, navigate to the start of the Banner area and press Enter to expand the “Main drawer” button.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “My calendars” button.
    • If the “My calendars” button is collapsed, press Enter.
  • Press Tab to get to the first calendar.
  • To find the calendar you want, use the Down arrow key.
  • To show or hide the calendar, press Space.
  • For additional options, press Tab.
View other calendars

You can add other calendars to your calendar.

  • Navigate to the Main drawer area with the Plus key.
    • If the Main drawer area isn’t available, navigate to the start of the Banner area and press Enter to expand the “Main drawer” button.
  • Press Tab until you get to the “Other calendars” button.
    • If the “Other calendars” button is collapsed, press Enter.
  • Press Tab to the “Add other calendars” button and press Enter.
  • Use the Up or Down arrow keys to select a menu option:
    • Subscribe
    • Browse
    • Create
  • Press Enter to add the calendar.
  • Press Tab to get to the first calendar.
  • To find the calendar you want, use the Down arrow key.
  • To show or hide the calendar, press Space.
  • For additional options, press Tab.
Identify which calendar an event is in

To find out who created an event or whose calendar the event is on:

  • Press e to open the event details and use virtual cursor or browse mode to navigate the dialog. The organizer is near the bottom.
  • Use arrow keys in virtual cursor off or focus mode. You’ll hear the calendar name as you highlight calendar entries.

Use Google Calendar with a screen reader (iPhone and iPad)

If you use a screen reader in Google Calendar, you can:

  • Create and track events
  • Send invites to others
  • Accept or reject invitations

Turn on iPhone or iPad accessibility features

If you haven’t used VoiceOver, go to the Apple support site to learn about accessibility features on your iPhone, iPad, and iPod touch.

Navigate in Google Calendar

The following navigation controls are at the top of your screen:

  • Month name: If you’re in schedule view, day view, or week view, to show or hide a date picker, double-tap the month name.
  • Jump to today: Double-tap to move your focus to today.
  • Views, calendars, and settings: Double-tap to open and explore this section, which includes:
    • Calendar views: Schedule, Day, 3 Day (on iPhone only), Week and Month.
    • Search
    • A list of your calendars
    • Settings
    • Help & Feedback
Create an event, reminder, or goal
  • Swipe to select “Create,” then double tap.
  • To select Event, Reminder, or Goal, swipe left or right.
  • Double-tap the screen.
  • Complete the event details.

Use navigation shortcuts in different calendar views

To learn how to navigate, choose a calendar view below:

Check your schedule of events, reminders, & goals

When you open your calendar, you’ll be in “Schedule view” by default.

  • Move between events, reminders, and goals: Swipe left or right.
  • Move to the previous or next day: Three-finger swipe up or down.
Check a single day schedule
  • Edit the focused event, reminder, or goal: Double-tap.
  • Move to the previous or next day: Three-finger swipe up or down.
  • Move to the next or previous event, reminder, or goal: In the hour grid, swipe right or left.
Check the next three days schedule
  • Move to the previous or next day: Three-finger swipe up or down.
  • Move focus to the first event, reminder, or goal of the day: Swipe right.
  • Move to the previous or next event, reminder, or goal: In the hour grid, swipe right or left.
  • Edit the focused event, reminder, or goal: Double-tap. Tip: This view is only available on iPhone.
Check the whole week schedule
  • Move to the previous or next week: Three-finger swipe left or right.
  • Move focus onto the first event, reminder, or goal of the day: Swipe right.
  • Move to the next or previous event, reminder, or goal: In the hour grid, swipe right or left.
  • Edit the focused event, reminder, or goal: Double-tap.
Check an event, reminder, or goal
  • Move to the previous or next part of the event, reminder, or goal: Swipe right or left.
  • Exit event, reminder, or goal: Two-finger swipe in a “Z” gesture.
Create or edit an event
  • Move to the previous or next field: Swipe right or left.
  • Exit the create or edit view: Two-finger swipe in a “Z” gesture.
Pick a date in date picker

In schedule view, day view, or week view, to pick from days in the month, double-tap the month name in the header.

Tips:

  • Move one day at a time: Swipe left or right, or drag your finger over the month.
  • Move to the next or previous month: Three-finger swipe up or down.
  • Open the currently focused day: Double-tap.
  • Exit the date picker: Two-finger swipe in a “Z” gesture.

Use keyboard shortcuts in Google Calendar

When you turn on keyboard shortcuts, you can press keys to make changes to your Google Calendar and quickly navigate to certain pages.

Turn on keyboard shortcuts

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right corner, click Settings Settings.
  • Check the box next to “Enable keyboard shortcuts.”
  • At the bottom of the page, click Save.

Use shortcuts

After you turn on keyboard shortcuts, you can see all the shortcuts you can use by typing a question mark ?.

View Tasks & Keep

You can now use Keep and Tasks in a side panel while using Calendar. To go to the side panel, use these shortcuts:

  • Windows: Ctrl + Alt + . (period) or Ctrl + Alt + , (comma)
  • Chromebook: Alt + Shift + . (period) or Alt + Shift + , (comma)
  • Mac: ⌘ + Option + . (period) or ⌘ + Option + , (comma)

View your day, week, or month (Computer)

Check your calendar to find out when you’re busy or available.

Change your calendar view

After you choose a new view, it becomes your default view until you change it.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, choose a view: Day, Week, Month, Year, Schedule, or 4 days.

Change view settings

Choose which day your calendar week starts, set a custom view, and use an alternate calendar.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right corner, click Settings Settings.
  • At the left, click View options.
  • Choose your settings. Changes are automatically saved. Important: The side-by-side calendars won’t work for the week or month view in Google Calendar.

Navigate your calendar

There are two ways to move between dates:

  • Use the arrows in the top left corner of the Calendar to move to recent dates in the past or future.
  • Use the small calendar in the top left corner to select a date. To return to today’s date, click Today in the top left corner.

Show shared calendars

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, choose which calendars you want to show:
    • My calendars: Calendars you own.
    • Other calendars in side-by-side day view: Primary calendars of shared people, rooms, or group calendars. Shared calendars must auto-accept invitations.
