View your day, week, or month (iPhone and iPad)

Check your calendar to find out when you’re busy or available.

Go to a specific day

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap the month name. For example, January .
  • Swipe left or right to go to other months.
  • Tap a date to find events on that day. Get back to today: At the top right corner, tap Jump to today .

Choose how many days to view

When you open the Calendar app, you’ll get a list of your upcoming events. You can switch views to find your whole day or multiple days.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • Choose an option:
    • iPhone: At the top left, tap Menu .
    • iPad: At the top right, find the option to change the view.
  • Choose a view, such as Schedule or Month. To find a list of all your events or tasks by day, choose Schedule.

Change your settings

Learn how to change your calendar settings, including the way your calendar looks and the day your week starts on.

Google Workspace Experiments

Get started with Google Workspace Experiments

To get early access to AI features in Google Workspace, you can sign up for Google Workspace Experiments with your personal account. Workspace Experiments is a trusted tester program which allows you to try out new AI features. Features in Workspace Experiments are in development, and their quality and availability may vary.

Sign up for Workspace Experiments

What you need

  • Be 18 years or older.
  • A personal Google Account that you manage.
    • Workspace Experiments is not available for Google Workspace accounts. Tips:
  • You can get access to Workspace Experiments features in some countries and languages. Learn where you can use Workspace Experiments features.
  • To check if you have access to Workspace Experiments, look for the Gemini icon in a Workspace app like Gmail or Docs.
    • For example, at the top right of Gmail or Docs, click Ask Gemini . If it’s present, you have access to features in Workspace Experiments.

Learn about current Workspace Experiments features

As a Workspace Experiments participant, you get early access to generative AI features that change over time. Some features roll out gradually and might not be available to you yet.

Gmail

Feature

Description

AI Overview in Gmail search

Generate a concise summary or answer from your emails directly above search results when you ask a natural language question.

Summarize long email conversations with key points and replies when you open a thread.

Use your voice to search and ask questions about your emails in the Gmail app.

Help me write

Draft new emails or refine existing text based on a prompt.

Proofread

Get writing suggestions beyond spelling and grammar for conciseness, tone, and style.

Suggested replies

Select detailed, context-aware reply suggestions at the bottom of an email thread.

Summarize and draft emails

Ask Gemini to summarize, draft, and suggest responses to emails.

Fix access issues with Workspace Experiments features

If you can’t access Workspace Experiments features, try the solutions below:

  • For English-only features, check that your language is set to English. Learn how to change the language for your Google Account.
  • Check if Workspace Experiments is available in your country. Learn more about where Workspace Experiments is available.
  • If you opt out of Workspace Experiments, you can’t access Workspace Experiments features.

Turn off Workspace Experiments features

To turn off Workspace Experiments features, you must exit Workspace Experiments. If you exit, you permanently lose access to all Workspace Experiments features, and you can’t rejoin Workspace Experiments.

To permanently exit Workspace Experiments, go to Google Docs, Google Slides, Sheets, Gmail, or Meet.

How to exit from Google Docs, Slides & Sheets

  • On your computer, open a file on Google Docs, Slides, or Sheets.

  • At the top, go to Tools Opt out of Workspace Experiments.

  • Click Confirm. How to exit from Gmail

  • On your computer, go to Gmail.

  • At the top, click Settings .

  • In the Workspace Experiments program notification, click Opt out.

  • Click Opt out.

  • Refresh your browser. How to exit from Meet

  • On your computer, go to Meet.

  • At the top right, click Settings .

  • On the left, click General.

  • Next to “You can use Workspace Experiments features,” click Opt out.

Organize & manage your tasks

Create & manage tasks in Google Calendar

In Google Calendar, you can create, view, and change tasks.

Create a task in Calendar

Important: Only work or school accounts can use the “Do not disturb” and “Automatically decline meetings” options.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On your calendar, click an empty slot.
    • Or, at the top left, you can click Create.
  • Click Task.
  • Update your task settings.
  • Click Save. Tip: To find a list of all tasks from the last 365 days, at the top of your calendar, click Pending tasks.
Learn about task settings

Task setting

Description

Start date and time

Enter the start date and how much time you plan to spend on the task.

Deadline

Enter the date you expect to complete the task. The deadline appears on the “All day” section of Calendar.

Description

Enter the date when the task must be completed.

Task list

Select a list for your task.

Advanced settings

Customize your task with additional options. You can:

  • Change the color of your tasks.
  • Set your availability to Free or Busy.
  • Turn on Do not disturb to mute chat notifications.
  • Turn on Automatically decline meetings if your availability is “Busy.”
  • Select your task’s visibility to others.

Create a task from Tasks in Calendar

  • On your computer, select an option:

    • Open Tasks in Calendar.
    • At the top right, click Tasks .

  • Click Create.

  • Enter your task details.

  • Click Save. Tips:

  • To add a task to an existing list, at the top, click Add a task.

  • Tasks with a date also appear on your calendar.

Mark a task as complete

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left of the task name, click Complete .
  • At the bottom right, click Mark completed.

View, edit, or delete tasks

To appear on your calendar, tasks must have a date.

View your tasks

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • On the left panel, go to “My calendars.”

    • To find “My calendars,” you may have to click Menu .

  • Make sure the “Tasks” box is checked. Edit a task

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Click the task you want to edit Edit task .

  • Update any task details.

  • Click Save. Tips:

  • You can also edit tasks from the side panel or from Tasks in Calendar.

  • If you want to change the date and time of the next repeating task in a series, click the date.

  • To edit the date and time for all tasks, click the repetition info. Edit a deadline

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Click the task you want to edit Edit task .

  • Next to “Deadline ,” enter a new due date for the task.

  • At the bottom right, click Save. Edit the task start & end time

Important: If you share your calendar with someone, you can change the visibility of tasks with a start and end time. Learn how to change event and task visibility.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Click the task you want to edit Edit task .

  • Next to “Start date ,” choose your start and end time.

  • At the bottom right, click Save. Tips:

  • You can’t add an end time for recurring tasks.

  • To convert an existing task into a task with a start and end time:

    • Open the task.
    • Click Add time. Delete a task

  • Open Google Calendar.

  • Click the task to update Delete . Add an attachment

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • At the bottom right, click Show side panel .

  • On the right, click Tasks .

  • To the right of the task, click Task options Add attachment.

  • From your Google Drive, select a file.

  • Click Insert. Learn more about attachments in Tasks.

Hide tasks on your calendar

Hide completed tasks

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • At the top right, next to “Settings menu ,” click the View switcher menu .

  • In the dropdown, deselect Show completed tasks. Hide all tasks

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • On the left panel, under “My calendars,” uncheck the box next to “Tasks.”

    • To find “My calendars,” click Menu .

Find & edit pending tasks

To check all uncompleted tasks from the last 365 days:

  • Open Google Calendar.
  • While viewing a day’s schedule, click Pending tasks.
  • In the pop-up list, choose how to update pending tasks:
    • To edit a task, hover to the right of the task. Click Edit , make your changes, and click Save.
    • To complete a task, hover to the far left of the task. Click Complete .
    • To reschedule a task, hover to the right of the task. Click Reschedule this task .

Learn about task notifications

  • If you add a date and time to your task, you get notifications at the scheduled date and time.
  • Tasks with a date also appear in the Calendar app.
  • For tasks without a time, notifications appear at 9 AM.
  • For tasks with a deadline, you get a notification to complete the task on the day of the deadline at 9 AM in your local time.

Learn about repeating tasks

  • To repeat a task, select the date and time Repeat .
  • Tasks with subtasks can’t repeat.
  • In Calendar, a certain number of upcoming instances of a repeating task will appear on the calendar grid, and as time passes, new ones will be added automatically.

Related resources

  • Organize your tasks
  • Add or edit a task

Create & manage tasks in Google Calendar (iPhone and iPad)

In Google Calendar, you can create, view, and change tasks.

Create a task in Calendar

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • On your calendar, tap an empty slot Task.
    • Or, you can tap Add Task .
  • Update your task settings.
  • Tap Save. Tip: To find a list of all tasks from the last 365 days, at the top of your calendar, tap Pending tasks.
Learn about task settings

Task setting

Description

Start date and time

Enter the task start date and the total task time.

Deadline

Enter the date you expect to complete the task. This date shows in the “All day” section of your calendar.

Description

Enter the task end date.

Task list

Select a list for your task.

View, edit, or delete tasks

View your tasks

Tasks must have a date to appear in Google Calendar. To turn on Tasks:

  • Go to the Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu .
  • Make sure the “Tasks” box is checked.
  • Any tasks with dates will appear on your calendar. Tip: A list of all uncompleted tasks from the last 365 days are shown as “Pending tasks,” which can be found in the current day.

Hide all tasks

  • Open the Google Calendar app ,

  • In the top left, tap Menu .

  • Uncheck the box next to “Tasks.” Edit a task

  • Open the Google Calendar app .

  • Tap a task to update Edit task .

  • Update task details.

  • Tap Save. Mark a task as complete

  • Open the Google Calendar app .

  • Tap a task to update Mark completed. Completed tasks are shown on your calendar by default.

Hide completed tasks

To hide completed tasks crossed out in your calendar, you can adjust your settings.

In your settings

  • On your iPhone & iPad, open the Google Calendar app .

  • Tap Menu Settings General.

  • Turn off Show completed tasks. Report an assigned task as spam

  • On your device, open the Google Calendar app .

  • On the calendar grid, tap the task you want to report.

  • At the top right, tap Options .

  • Tap Report as spam Report as spam. Learn how to report assigned tasks as spam

Delete a task

When you delete a task from your calendar, it’s removed completely.

  • Open the Google Calendar app .
  • Tap the task to delete More Delete Delete task. Find & edit pending tasks

To check all uncompleted tasks from the last 365 days:

  • Open the Google Calendar app .
  • While viewing a day’s schedule, tap Pending tasks.
  • In the pop-up list, choose how to update pending tasks:
    • To edit a task, tap the task, tap the pencil icon , make your changes, and tap Save.
    • To delete a task, tap the task and tap the , then tap Delete.
    • To complete a task, swipe right.

Learn about task notifications

  • If you add a date and time to your task, you get notifications at the scheduled date and time.
  • Tasks with a date also appear in the Calendar app.
  • For tasks without a time, notifications appear at 9 AM.
  • For tasks with a deadline, you get a notification to complete the task on the day of the deadline at 9 AM in your local time.

Learn about repeating tasks

  • To repeat a task, select the date and time Repeat .
  • Tasks with subtasks can’t repeat.
  • In Calendar, a certain number of upcoming instances of a repeating task will appear on the calendar grid, and as time passes, new ones will be added automatically.

Learn about Google Keep reminders in Google Tasks

In the second half of 2025, Google Keep reminders will migrate to Google Tasks. New reminders will be saved as tasks. You can view, edit, and complete your reminders from:

  • Calendar
  • Gemini
  • Keep
  • Tasks

Find Keep reminders in Calendar & Tasks

You can find your Keep reminders in:

  • Your calendar grid in Calendar

  • Your task list Tips:

  • To identify a Keep reminder, in the task details, find “From Keep.”

  • To return to the original Keep note, select From Keep.

Get reminder notifications on your mobile device

You no longer get reminder notifications from Google Keep. To receive reminder notifications on your mobile device, install one of these apps:

  • Calendar
  • Tasks After you install an app on your device, make sure to allow notifications for it. Learn how to manage Google Tasks notification settings.

Edit & delete Keep reminders

You can manage Keep reminders from several Google products.

  • To edit the date and time of a reminder, make changes in the:
    • Keep note
    • Task details in Tasks or Calendar
  • To edit the title of a reminder, use Tasks or Calendar.
    • If you only change the title of the Keep note, the reminder’s title doesn’t update.
  • You can mark reminders as complete in the:
    • Reminder notification on your mobile device
    • Keep note
    • Task details in Tasks or Calendar

Learn what to expect when your Keep reminders move to Tasks

In general, Keep reminders move to Tasks with the same data and are visible on your Keep note and in Tasks. Here are some key points to remember when your Keep reminders move to Tasks:

  • Location-based reminders in Keep: You can no longer create or get location-based reminders. To find your location reminder data:
    • On your device, open the Tasks app .
    • Select a Keep reminder that was moved to Tasks.
    • Review the description.
  • Limits on tasks: You can have up to 100,000 tasks. If the number of Keep reminders is more than the task limit, the oldest reminders don’t migrate to Tasks.
  • Long reminders: If your Keep reminder is too long, its title is shortened after it migrates to Tasks.
  • Pending tasks: You can find a list of all “Pending tasks” from the last 365 days in the “All-day” section of your calendar for the current day.
  • Repeating tasks: The maximum interval for repeating tasks in Calendar is 1000 days, weeks, months, or years. For example, if you set a task to repeat every 2,000 days, Calendar changes it to every 1,000 days.
    • Reminders that don’t repeat and are older than a year are added to an “Old Google Keep Reminders” list.
  • Tasks beyond the year 3000: Any tasks with dates beyond the year 3000 are adjusted to the year 2900.
  • Work or school accounts managed by an administrator:
    • If you’re an administrator, make sure that the “Tasks service” is turned on for your organization. Learn how to turn on Tasks for your organization.
    • If the “Tasks service” is off, your organization’s reminder data doesn’t migrate to Tasks.
    • Reminders for users with suspended Workspace licenses don’t migrate to Tasks.
    • If your organization allows Google Takeout, you can export your reminders data before it’s deleted.

Related resources

  • Set up reminders for your notes
  • Learn about Google Tasks
  • Manage Google Tasks notification settings
  • Create & manage tasks in Google Calendar
  • Use Google products side by side

Manage repeating tasks in Google Tasks & Google Calendar

You can create, edit, and delete repeating tasks in Google Tasks and Google Calendar.

Important:

  • A limited number of upcoming recurring tasks appear at one time on your Calendar grid. As time passes, new tasks appear automatically.
  • Shared tasks and subtasks can’t be set to repeat.
  • Recurring tasks can’t be moved to other task lists.
  • If you stop a series of tasks from repeating, you can’t set it to repeat again. You must create a new series of repeating tasks.

Set tasks to repeat

To set tasks to repeat from Google Tasks:
  • Go to Gmail, Calendar, Chat, Drive, or a file in Google Docs, Sheets, or Slides.
  • On the right, click Tasks .
  • Click an existing task or create a new task.
  • To add a date and time, click Date/time.
  • Next to “Date/time,” click Repeat .
  • Under “Repeats every,” select day, week, month, or year.
  • Under “Ends,” select an option to create a recurring task:
    • With no end date, select Never.
    • With a specific end date, select On, then choose a date.
    • That ends after a certain number of occurrences, select After, then select a number of occurrences.
  • Click OK.
To set tasks to repeat from Google Calendar:
  • Open Google Calendar.
  • On the calendar grid, click any empty space.
  • Below the title, select Task.
  • Below the date, click Does not repeat.
  • Select an option:
    • Select a pre-set frequency, such as Daily, Weekly, Monthly, or Annually. Click Save.
    • Select Custom, then set your preferred recurrence frequency or an end date. Click Done Save.

Edit or complete a recurring task

To edit or complete a recurring task from Google Tasks:
  • Go to Gmail, Calendar, Chat, Drive, or a file in Google Docs, Sheets, or Slides.
  • On the right, click Tasks .
  • Click the task you want to change.
    • To edit the date and time for all tasks in a series, at the bottom of the task, click the repetition info.
    • To change the date and time of the next repeating task in a series, click the date.
    • To stop a task from repeating, next to the repetition info, click Stop repeating Stop repeating.
    • To mark the task complete, on the left of a task, click Complete . At the bottom of the panel, under “Completed,” you can view your completed tasks. The next instance of your repeating task appears in your task list on the date specified.
To edit or complete a recurring task from Google Calendar:
  • Open Google Calendar.
  • Click a task you want to change.
  • To edit, click Edit .
  • Select an option:
    • To edit the date or time, click Date/Time .
    • To edit how often a task occurs or stop a task from repeating, click Repeat .
  • Click Save.
  • Choose between applying changes to “This task” or “All tasks.”
  • Click OK.
  • To mark the task complete, click Mark completed.

Delete tasks in a series

To delete all tasks in a series from Google Tasks:
  • Go to Gmail, Calendar, Chat, Drive, or a file in Google Docs, Sheets, or Slides.
  • On the right, click Tasks .
  • Next to the task you want to delete, click Open menu .
  • Click Delete Delete all.
To delete tasks in a series from Google Calendar:
  • Open Google Calendar.
  • Click the task you want to delete.
  • Click Delete .
  • Select an option:
    • This task
    • This and following tasks
    • All tasks
  • Click OK. Important: To delete a single task from a repeating series, you must use Google Calendar.

Manage repeating tasks in Google Tasks & Google Calendar (Computer)

You can create, edit, and delete repeating tasks in Google Tasks and Google Calendar.

Important:

  • A limited number of upcoming recurring tasks appear at one time on your Calendar grid. As time passes, new tasks appear automatically.
  • Shared tasks and subtasks can’t be set to repeat.
  • Recurring tasks can’t be moved to other task lists.
  • If you stop a series of tasks from repeating, you can’t set it to repeat again. You must create a new series of repeating tasks.

Set tasks to repeat

To set tasks to repeat from Google Tasks:
  • Go to Gmail, Calendar, Chat, Drive, or a file in Google Docs, Sheets, or Slides.
  • On the right, click Tasks .
  • Click an existing task or create a new task.
  • To add a date and time, click Date/time.
  • Next to “Date/time,” click Repeat .
  • Under “Repeats every,” select day, week, month, or year.
  • Under “Ends,” select an option to create a recurring task:
    • With no end date, select Never.
    • With a specific end date, select On, then choose a date.
    • That ends after a certain number of occurrences, select After, then select a number of occurrences.
  • Click OK.
To set tasks to repeat from Google Calendar:
  • Open Google Calendar.
  • On the calendar grid, click any empty space.
  • Below the title, select Task.
  • Below the date, click Does not repeat.
  • Select an option:
    • Select a pre-set frequency, such as Daily, Weekly, Monthly, or Annually. Click Save.
    • Select Custom, then set your preferred recurrence frequency or an end date. Click Done Save.

    • To mark the task complete, on the left of a task, click Complete . At the bottom of the panel, under “Completed,” you can view your completed tasks. The next instance of your repeating task appears in your task list on the date specified.

Report assigned tasks as spam

You can report an assigned task as spam from a shared surface, like a space or document.

When you report an assigned task as spam, it’s:

  • Removed from your personal list.
  • No longer assigned to you in the shared space.

Report an assigned task as spam in Google Tasks

  • Go to Gmail, Calendar, Chat, Drive, or a file in Google Docs, Sheets, or Slides.
  • In the side panel, on the right, click Tasks .
  • Click the task you want to report.
  • To the right of the task, click Open menu .
  • Click Report as spam Report as spam.

Report an assigned task as spam in Google Calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the calendar grid, click the task you want to report.
  • At the top right of the task, click Options .
  • Click Report as spam Report as spam. Tip: To report an assigned task as spam, enter the keyboard shortcut: !

Schedule events in Gmail

Insert an event or booking page in a Gmail message

Important: To use this feature, you must have a Workspace Business Starter subscription or a personal Gmail account. If you have another Workspace subscription or Google AI plan, you can use the “Help me schedule” feature in Gmail. Learn how to suggest times to meet with Gemini in Gmail.

When you draft a reply or a new email, you can insert an event or booking page directly into the email.

Create an event in a Gmail message

Important:

  • Only the subject of your email is added to the event title.

  • To add the body of your email to the event, copy and paste the email’s text into the event description.

  • On your computer, go to Gmail.

  • Create or reply to an email.

  • At the bottom, click Set up a time to meet Create an event.

    • If you can’t find “Set up a time to meet,” at the bottom of the message, click More options .

  • On the right, confirm the event details.

  • To invite more people, add their email addresses.

  • Click Save Send. Tips:

  • When you create an event, it automatically adds an event summary back into Gmail.

  • If you delete the event summary from the message, the event isn’t deleted from Google Calendar.

Insert a booking page in a Gmail message

If you have an appointment schedule from Calendar, you can insert a link to your booking page in a message. Learn about appointment schedules in Google Calendar.

  • On your computer, go to Gmail.
  • Create or reply to an email.
  • At the bottom, click Set up a time to meet .
    • If you can’t find “Set up a time to meet,” at the bottom of the message, click More options .
  • Choose a booking page.
    • If you don’t have an appointment schedule, click Create a booking page.

Learn how to schedule events in Gmail

In Gmail, you can schedule Google Calendar events and negotiate times to meet directly from a message. There are different options available in Gmail based on your account type.

Feature

Description

Availability

Suggest times to meet with Gemini in Gmail

Use Gemini in Gmail to coordinate and set up meetings with one or more people. If you have an appointment schedule, you can also insert a booking page.

This feature supports 1:1 and group meetings.

  • Workspace Business Standard and Plus
  • Workspace Enterprise Starter, Standard, and Plus
  • Workspace Frontline Plus
  • Google AI Pro for Education
  • Google AI Pro and Ultra
  • Gemini Business and Enterprise - Legacy Create an event from a Gmail message

Use Gmail to convert an existing email thread into a Calendar invite. When you create the event, it automatically copies the email subject and body into the event details, and adds all email recipients as invitees.

All Gmail users

Insert an event or booking page in a Gmail message

When you draft a reply or a new email, you can create an event and insert a summary of it in your email draft. If you have an appointment schedule, you can also insert a booking page.

  • Workspace Business Starter
  • Personal Gmail accounts Offer times to meet in a Gmail message

You can offer a list of times to meet from your calendar to your recipient. The event isn’t created until the recipient selects a time. Recipients get times in their own time zone on your booking page.

This feature supports 1:1 meetings only.

  • Workspace Business Starter
  • Personal Gmail accounts

Offer times to meet in a Gmail message

Important: To use this feature, you must have a Workspace Business Starter subscription or a personal Gmail account. If you have another Workspace subscription or Google AI plan, you can use the “Help me schedule” feature in Gmail. Learn how to suggest times to meet with Gemini in Gmail.

To schedule meetings, you can send your available times in a Gmail message. After the recipient selects a time, Gmail:

  • Automatically creates the meeting and adds it to your calendar.
  • Sends a confirmation email with the meeting details.

Learn about scheduling meetings

  • You can use this for 1:1 meetings. If your email has multiple recipients, only the first person who books can join the event.
    • You must add additional participants separately.
  • When you offer time slots you’re available to meet, others can book them for 30 days through the booking page.
  • By default, Calendar sets the meeting’s permissions to “See guest list.” You can modify guest permissions after the recipient confirms the event.
  • The proposed times in the Gmail message are shown in the event creator’s timezone. The proposed times in the recipient’s Gmail message are shown in their timezone.
    • Once created, the event shows on Calendar in the local timezone. Confirmation emails with event details show in the participant’s timezone.
  • You can only schedule meeting times on your primary calendar.

Insert your availability in a Gmail message

  • On your computer, go to Gmail.
  • Create or reply to an email.
  • At the bottom of your message, click Set up a time to meet Propose times you’re free.
    • Tip: If you can’t find “Set up a time to meet,” at the bottom of the message, click More options .
  • On the right, select the event duration.
  • Choose multiple times in the calendar grid. You can select times across different days.
  • Click Next.
  • Add an event title, description, and conference option.
  • Click Add to email Send.

Suggest times to meet with Gemini in Gmail

Important: This feature requires an eligible Google Workspace or Google AI plan. Learn about Gemini features and plans.

To set up meetings with one or more people in Gmail, use the “Help me schedule” feature when you draft a message or reply. Based on you and your guests’ availability, Gemini in Gmail generates a list of times when you’re all available.

Insert suggested times in a Gmail message

  • On your computer, go to Gmail.

  • Create or reply to an email with one or more recipients.

  • At the bottom of your message, click Help me schedule .

    • If you can’t find “Help me schedule,” click More options Help me schedule.

  • Review the list of suggested times.

  • At the bottom of the email, click Send. Tips:

  • A maximum of 20 guests can be added to a single schedule.

  • The guest list isn’t updated if the list of email recipients changes after the card is inserted. You need to re-insert the schedule.

  • Gemini in Gmail proposes 4 slots by default.

Update the suggested times card

  • Hover over Edit in Calendar .
  • Select an option:
    • Meeting duration: Change the length of the meeting.
    • Time range: To search for available meeting times, update the time range.
    • Change to text format: Send your meeting times as text instead of a suggestion card.
    • More options: Insert a booking page from your appointment schedule.

Update suggested times based on guest calendars

You can manually review suggested times based on guest calendars.

  • In the suggested times card, click Edit in Calendar .
  • In the “Share your availability” window, you can:
    • Check availability: You can check the availability of any guest’s calendar that you have access to.
    • Change meeting duration: Change the length of the meeting.
    • Add guest time zone: Include the guest’s time zone.
    • Add or remove guests: On the right, add or remove your guests. Suggestions automatically update based on the new guest list.
    • Review time slots: If calendars are busy, Gemini in Gmail can suggest a mix of times.
      • If the time slots are green, all guests are available to meet.
      • If the time slots are amber, there’s a conflict for one or more guests. If this happens, a banner at the top of the window prompts you to manually select time slots. To make sure the chosen time works for your priority guests, review the calendar grid.
  • At the bottom right, click Propose times.

Book time on someone else’s calendar

If you receive suggested times from someone else, you can book a time slot with them. Once booked, a Calendar event is created and all guests receive an event invitation.

  • In the email, select a suggested time.
  • On the “Book your preferred time” page, you can:
    • Confirm the event time: If there are no conflicts with other guests, click Book time for all guests.
    • Look for different times to meet: If there are conflicts, click View all times on calendar.
      • In the window, select a new time slot.

Learn about Gemini feature suggestions

  • Gemini feature suggestions don’t represent Google’s views, and should not be attributed to Google.
  • Don’t rely on Gemini features as medical, legal, financial or other professional advice.
  • Gemini features may suggest inaccurate or inappropriate information. Your feedback makes Gemini more helpful and safe.
  • Enterprise end users can submit feedback about their experience using this feature. End users are informed before they submit feedback that the feedback data shouldn’t contain personal, sensitive, or confidential information.

  • Get started with Google Workspace with Gemini

Set up & create appointments

Cancel appointments on your calendar

When you need to make changes to your availability, you can cancel appointments on your calendar. If you no longer wish to use an existing appointment schedule, you can delete it.

Cancel a booked appointment

Important: When you cancel a booked appointment:

  • The appointment is removed from your calendar.
  • A cancellation email is sent to the person who booked the appointment.
  • The canceled appointment time appears available on your booking page.
  • On your computer, open the booked appointment on Google Calendar.
    • To cancel a third-party calendar appointment, use the link in your confirmation email or decline the calendar invitation.
  • Click Cancel appointment.
  • On the booking page that opens, click Cancel your appointment. Tip: If you or the person who booked an appointment with you forwards the appointment confirmation email to someone, the recipient can cancel the booked appointment.

Delete an appointment schedule

Important: When you delete an appointment schedule:

  • The appointment schedule is removed from your calendar.
  • Booked appointments stay on your calendar and the person’s calendar who booked the appointment.
  • Links to booking pages you shared no longer work.
  • On a computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click the appointment schedule you want to delete.
  • At the top right, in the appointment window, click Delete appointment schedule . Tip: To make changes to your appointment schedule, like removing a time block, edit the appointment schedule.

Cancel appointments on your calendar (Computer)

When you need to make changes to your availability, you can cancel appointments on your calendar. If you no longer wish to use an existing appointment schedule, you can delete it.

Important: When you cancel a booked appointment:

  • The appointment is removed from your calendar.
  • A cancellation email is sent to the person who booked the appointment.
  • The canceled appointment time appears available on your booking page.
  • On your computer, open the booked appointment on Google Calendar.
    • To cancel a third-party calendar appointment, use the link in your confirmation email or decline the calendar invitation.
  • Click Cancel appointment.
  • On the booking page that opens, click Cancel your appointment. Tip: If you or the person who booked an appointment with you forwards the appointment confirmation email to someone, the recipient can cancel the booked appointment.

Cancel appointments on your calendar (iPhone and iPad)

When you need to make changes to your availability, you can cancel appointments on your calendar. If you no longer wish to use an existing appointment schedule, you can delete it.

Cancel a booked appointment

Important: When you cancel a booked appointment:

  • The appointment is removed from your calendar.
  • A cancellation email is sent to the person who booked the appointment.
  • The canceled appointment time appears available on your booking page.
  • Open your appointment confirmation email.
  • Tap Cancel appointment.
  • On the new page, tap Cancel your appointment Confirm Close.

Check your availability across calendars

Important: To check availability from secondary calendars, you need to have an eligible Google Workspace or Google One plan. Compare premium features for appointment schedules.

To prevent conflicts with other events, you can check your availability across multiple calendars. This includes your primary calendar and any calendars that you own, manage, or are subscribed to.

Learn how Calendar checks for conflicts

  • When you check other calendars for availability:
    • Appointment times during events on your calendar that are set to “Busy” don’t show on the booking page. Learn how to change event privacy settings.
    • No appointment times show up on days where you have an all-day event set to “Busy.”
  • When you don’t check other calendars for availability:
    • Appointment times show up on your booking page during times where you have a “Busy” event on your calendar.
    • All time blocks appear as open on your booking pages. This applies even when you accept a non-appointment event. Each appointment time on a booking page can only be booked once, even with this feature turned off.
    • If someone books an appointment, Google Calendar removes that appointment time from the booking page.
  • If you RSVP “Yes” or “Maybe” to an event or have any busy event in a calendar that is reviewed for conflicts, that block of time doesn’t appear as open on your booking page.

Review secondary calendars for conflicts

  • On a computer, open Google Calendar.

  • Click an existing appointment schedule Edit appointment schedule .

  • Click Calendars.

  • Select the calendars you’d like to check for conflicts.

  • To check the impact of your appointment schedule, your calendar shows a preview of your selected calendars.

  • If you don’t want to check for schedule conflicts, turn off Check calendars for availability. Tips:

  • If an appointment schedule is hidden and you create or edit a schedule, the hidden appointment schedule appears again. Booked appointments are never hidden on the Calendar grid.

  • When the schedule is created on a shared calendar, it may not be possible to select other calendars to be reviewed for conflicts.

Fix availability issues on your booking page

  • Check your scheduling window settings: These settings limit how far in advance or how close to the actual appointment time bookers can book an appointment. If appointment times are outside the set ranges, they don’t show on the booking page.
  • Check booked appointment settings: These settings manage the booked appointments that appear on your calendar. If you set up a “Maximum bookings per day,” appointment times may not show up if it reaches the set limit. Tip: No matter which setting you choose, each appointment time on a booking page can only be booked once.

Compare premium features for appointment schedules

Important: For Workspace Frontline, Essentials, or an older Google Workspace subscription that’s no longer offered, you can’t create appointment schedules.

With a personal Google Account or Workspace Business Starter plan, you can create a single booking page to let others schedule appointments on your calendar.

Some features require an eligible Google Workspace or Google One subscription. Use the chart to understand the features available for a subscription type.

Create more than one appointment schedule

Create an appointment schedule

When you create a new appointment schedule, you need to:

  • Select the appointment duration and your availability.
  • Customize how you meet with others.

Before you begin

Here’s what you need to create an appointment schedule:

A personal Google Account or Workspace account. For help with work or school accounts, contact your administrator.

A computer with a supported web browser. You can’t create an appointment schedule in the Calendar app for Android or iPhone and iPad.

Tips:

  • Some appointment schedule features require an eligible Google Workspace or Google One subscription. Compare premium features available for appointment schedules.
  • For eligible Workspace accounts, Calendar automatically generates a booking page. This page is set up based on your weekday availability and doesn’t show times when you’re busy.

Step 1: Set up your appointment schedule

  • On a computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top left, click Create.
  • Select Appointment schedule.
  • On the left, in the “Bookable appointment schedule” panel, set up your appointment settings.
  • Click Next. Tip: To set up your appointment schedule in the “Booking pages” section, click Create appointment schedule .

Appointment setting

Description

Add title

Enter a title for your appointment schedule.

  • The title is visible to anyone who has the link to your booking page.

  • The title appears on your calendar for the schedule and incoming bookings. Appointment duration

  • Under “Appointment duration,” click the Down arrow .

  • Select the duration.

    • To set a custom duration, select Custom. General availability

Set the date, time, and time zone of your appointments.

For appointments that repeat weekly, next to a time slot, select:

  • Unavailable all day : Make yourself unavailable for the selected time slot.
  • Add another period to this day : Add another time slot on the same day.
  • Copy time to all : Apply the same schedule to all days. Scheduling window

Limit the time range that people can schedule appointments on your calendar, which includes:

  • The maximum time in advance others can schedule an appointment with you.
  • The minimum lead time you require for others to schedule an appointment with you. Tip: By default, there is a 4-hour minimum lead time to schedule an appointment. Because of this, if you check your booking page immediately after setting it up, you might not see any availability for the current day.

Adjusted availability

Change your availability for specific dates, like holidays.

Booked appointment settings

Manage the appointments that appear on your calendar.

  • Buffer time: Add time between appointments on your calendar.

  • Maximum bookings per day: Limit the number of appointments you accept in a single day.

  • Guest permissions: Allow guests to invite others to your appointments. Calendars

  • Select a primary or secondary calendar for your appointments.

  • To avoid schedule conflicts, select Check calendars for availability.

  • You can’t add appointments to resource calendars. Learn how to share room and resource calendars. Co-hosts

  • Enter co-host names or email addresses.

  • You can also add Google Groups directly.

  • As a co-host, you can’t add groups or users outside of your domain. Tip: Include co-hosts’ calendars in availability checks to prevent double-bookings. By default, appointment schedules do not check co-host availability.

Step 2: Set up your booking page

  • On the left, in the “Bookable appointment schedule” panel, set up your booking page settings.
  • Click Save. Booking page setting

Description

Booking page photo and name

Check how your identity is shown on your booking page. For secondary calendars, booking pages don’t show a photo for appointment schedules.

To change the profile photo or account name on your booking page, update your personal info in your Google Account.

Location and conferencing

To select how and where you meet people who book appointments with you:

  • Click Select how and where to meet.
  • Select:
    • Google Meet video conferencing
    • In-person meeting
    • Phone call
    • None / to be specified later Tip: To use a third-party conference service, select None / to be specified later. When someone books an appointment, edit the event and add a link to your preferred conferencing service. Learn how to install Calendar add-ons.

Description

Add a description that explains your service. The description displays:

  • On your booking page
  • In confirmation emails
  • In event descriptions Booking form

Add fields for attendees to fill out, which includes required fields for:

  • First name
  • Last name
  • Email address To add more fields, click Add an item.

To avoid spam bookings, check the box next to Require email verification. This sends a verification code to people not signed in to a Google Account when they request an appointment.

Payments & cancellation policy

After you connect a Stripe account to Calendar, you can request payment for appointments. Learn how to require payments for appointments.

Booking confirmations and reminders

Set up confirmation emails and reminders for your appointments.

  • You can send up to 5 reminders.
  • You can’t edit the text of the reminder emails.
  • To send more than one reminder, click Add reminder.
Ready to begin?

Create your first appointment schedule

Edit your appointment schedule

To adjust your availability or make other changes, you can edit your appointment schedule.

Make changes to your appointment schedule

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “Booking pages,” hover over the appointment schedule you want to edit.
  • Click Options Edit.
  • Make changes to your appointment schedule.

Add availability to an appointment schedule

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the calendar, click any open time slot.
  • Click Appointment schedule.
  • Add a date and time.
  • Click Add availability to an existing schedule.
  • Select the schedule where you want to add your availability.
  • Click Add to existing schedule.

Show or hide your appointment schedules on your calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “Booking pages,” hover over the appointment schedule you want to show or hide.
  • Click Options
    • To show an appointment schedule: Click Show on calendar.
    • To hide an appointment schedule: Click Hide from calendar. Tip: If your appointment schedule is hidden, you can:
  • Still find booked appointments on your calendar.
  • Create a new schedule or add time to an available one.
  • Still allow others to book appointments with you.

Learn about schedule updates, visibility & emails

Learn how your appointment schedule syncs with your calendar

  • Your appointment schedule and booked appointments automatically display on your calendar.

  • When you select “Check calendars for availability,” Google Calendar avoids schedule conflicts.

  • If you RSVP “Yes” or “Maybe” to an event in Google Calendar, that block of time won’t display as open on your booking page.

  • To avoid conflicts with other events, your booking page availability automatically updates. Learn how appointment schedule visibility works

  • Anyone with edit permission on your calendar can edit the schedule and find new bookings on your calendar.

  • Anyone with your booking page link can check for your available time slots.

  • If you share your booking page, but not your calendar, no one can find events on your calendar.

  • On your booking pages, if you turn off “Check calendars for availability,” all appointment time blocks show as open. However, past appointment bookings and non-appointment events accepted through booking pages remain.

    • Even if the feature is off, others can book each appointment slot on a booking page only once. Get confirmation emails for bookings & cancellations
Appointment bookings
  • For schedules on the primary calendar: You, co-hosts, and the person who booked the appointment get an email notification.
  • For schedules on the secondary calendar: The co-hosts and the person who booked the appointment get an email notification.
Appointment cancellations
  • For canceled appointments on the primary calendar: You, co-hosts, and the person who booked the appointment get an email notification.
  • For canceled appointments on the secondary calendar: The co-hosts and the person who booked the appointment get an email notification. If you aren’t a co-host, set up your secondary calendar to receive email notifications for cancellations. Learn how to modify Google Calendar notifications.

Edit your appointment schedule (iPhone and iPad)

Show or hide your appointment schedules on your calendar

Important: To make changes to your appointment schedule, go to Google Calendar on your web browser.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu .
  • Tap your Appointment schedule.
  • Tap Options Show on calendar or Hide from calendar.

Learn about appointment schedules in Google Calendar

With appointment schedules, you can manage your availability and let people book time with you directly in Google Calendar. You can:

  • Create a booking page that contains the times you’re available.
  • Let others know when they can book appointments by adjusting your availability.
  • Share a link to your booking page or embed it on your website.

Learn how appointment schedules work

  • You create and share your booking page with others.
  • When someone books an appointment, it’s added to your calendar.
  • To avoid conflicts with other events on your calendar, your booking page blocks off times when you’re not available.

Ready to begin?

Create your first appointment schedule

Require payments for appointments

To simplify payments and prevent last minute cancellations, you can ask customers to pay in advance on your appointment schedule in Google Calendar.

Learn how paid appointments work

  • Paid appointment bookings are a premium feature for appointment schedules and available to users with an eligible Google Workspace subscription. Compare premium features for appointment schedules.
  • To require payments, you need to connect Google Calendar to a Stripe account.
  • When customers choose a time slot on your booking page, they checkout and pay for the appointment using Stripe to complete the booking process.
  • Customers can only select credit card payment, Apple Pay, and Google Pay as payment options.
  • Currencies are provided by Stripe. Learn about accepted currencies on Stripe.
  • If your Google Workspace account is managed by an organization, your administrator controls access to paid appointment bookings. If you don’t see this feature, contact your administrator.

Connect Stripe to Google Calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings menu Settings.
  • On the left, click General Appointment schedules.
  • Click Connect Stripe. Connect Stripe in Calendar settings

Set up your payments & cancellation policy

Important: As the event organizer, it’s your responsibility to carry out your cancellation and refund policies.

  • In Google Calendar, create or edit an appointment schedule.
  • Expand the “Payments & cancellation policy” section.
  • Check the box next to “Require payment when booking.”
  • Enter the amount and currency required for the appointment.
  • Optional: Add your cancellation policy.
    • The cancellation policy appears on your booking form and in email reminders.
  • Click Save. Create an appointment schedule

Disconnect Stripe from Google Calendar

Important: After you disconnect Stripe, all appointment schedules no longer require payment.

  • In Google Calendar, at the top right, click Settings menu Settings.
  • On the left, click General Appointment schedules.
  • Select the option to disconnect Stripe.
  • To confirm, click Disconnect Stripe.

Learn about payments & refunds for appointments

All payments and refunds are handled by Stripe. Google Calendar does not:

  • Process or store any payment info, such as credit card numbers

  • Charge any platform fees

  • Assist with payment or refund issues When you cancel an appointment as the organizer or the customer, a refund isn’t processed automatically.

  • If you’re the organizer: To refund the customer, go to your Stripe dashboard.

  • If you’re the customer: To request a refund for an appointment cancellation, review the organizer’s cancellation policy first, then contact them to ask for a refund.

Learn about information shared between Stripe & Calendar

After you connect Stripe to Google Calendar, some appointment information is shared with Stripe, including:

  • Price
  • Appointment title
  • Time slot To manage the data that Stripe uses, go to your Stripe dashboard. If you no longer want to use Stripe, disconnect Stripe from Google Calendar.

Share your appointment schedule

When you create a booking page, you can share a link on your website or through online communications that link to:

  • Your appointment booking page

  • All booking pages on your primary calendar Your booking page is always public. Anyone with the link can view your:

  • Booking page

  • Profile photo

  • Account name Tip: To find schedules on secondary calendars, you need to turn on the calendar in the calendar list. This helps you access the schedule in your calendar and doesn’t affect the visibility of the booking page.

Share an appointment schedule link

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, under “Booking pages,” hover over the booking page you want to share.
  • Click Copy link .

Add an appointment schedule button to your website

Important: If your account isn’t eligible to create multiple appointment schedules, you won’t get the option to choose between all booking pages or a single booking page. You can only share one booking page. Learn more about premium features for appointment schedules.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Under “Booking pages,” hover over a booking page.
  • Click Options Sharing options Website embed.
  • Select an option:
    • To share a link for all services on your calendar: Click All booking pages.
    • To share a link for a specific service on your calendar: Click A single booking page.
  • Click Button with popup.
    • Under “Button text,” type what you want your button to display.
    • Edit the color of the button.
  • Click Copy code Done.
  • Paste the code into your website’s HTML.

Embed the booking page on your website

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Under “Booking pages,” hover over a booking page.
  • Click Options Sharing options Website embed.
  • Select an option:
    • To share a link for all services on your calendar: Click All booking pages.
    • To share a link for a specific service on your calendar: Click A single booking page.
  • Click Inline booking page.
    • Optional: In the embed code, you can edit the width and height of your booking page.
  • Click Copy code Done.
  • Paste the code into your website’s HTML. Embed options available for website providers

When you create a booking page, you can share a link on your website or through online communications that link to:

Share an appointment schedule

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Google Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu .
  • Tap your appointment schedule Share.
  • Select an option:
    • To copy the URL, tap Copy link.
    • To share through another app, tap the app. Tip: To access a booking page that shows all services on your calendar, tap More options See all booking pages.

Update your availability for appointments

You can customize your appointment schedule to make it easier for people to view your availability.

Tip: Unaccepted or tentative Google Calendar events do not block appointment availability.

Set appointment schedule start & end dates

Important: You can only set start and end dates for recurring appointment schedules. Learn how to set a recurring appointment schedule.

You can choose the dates you want your appointment schedule to start and end.

  • On a computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an existing appointment schedule Edit appointment schedule .
  • Click Scheduling window Start and end dates.
  • Choose the dates you want your appointment schedule to start and end.
  • Click Done.
  • To save the updated schedule, click Next Save.

Change scheduling window and minimum and maximum notice settings

By default, appointment schedules have a 4-hour minimum notice to schedule an appointment. If you check your booking page immediately after setting it up, you might not see any availability for the current day because of this minimum lead time.

  • On a computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an existing appointment schedule Edit appointment schedule .
  • Click Scheduling window. Here you can update:
    • Maximum time in advance that an appointment can be booked.
    • Minimum time before the appointment starts that it can be booked.
  • Adjust the maximum or minimum time required for someone to book an appointment with you.
  • To save the updated schedule, click Next Save.

Change a date’s availability

You can choose specific dates where you’re more or less available than usual.

  • On a computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an existing appointment schedule Edit appointment schedule .
  • Under “Adjusted availability,” click Change a date’s availability.
  • Choose the date you want to adjust.
  • Change the time range to show the times you’re available.
    • If you’re not available that date at all, click Unavailable all day .
    • To add more than one available time range, click Add a time .
    • To remove a date exception, next to the date and time, click Remove .
  • To save the updated schedule, click Next Save. Tip: You can adjust availability for dates that are outside of a recurring schedule with start and end dates.

Manage booked appointment settings

To avoid back-to-back appointments, you can turn on and adjust buffer time.

  • On a computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Click an existing appointment schedule Edit appointment schedule .
  • Click Booked appointment settings.
  • To add time between appointments:
    • Next to “Buffer time,” check the box.
    • Enter the buffer time.
  • To limit the number of appointments to accept in a day:
    • Next to “Maximum bookings per day,” check the box.
    • Enter the maximum number of appointments to accept per day.
  • To save the updated schedule, click Next Save.

Verify emails for appointments

To prevent unwanted bookings, you can ask guests without a Google Account to verify their email before they book an appointment.

Learn how email verification works

  • To use email verification for appointment schedules, you need to have an eligible Google Workspace subscription. Learn more about premium features for appointment schedules.
  • By default, email verification is off.
  • When you sign in to a Google Account and make an appointment:
    • Your booking details are pre-filled.
    • You aren’t asked to submit a PIN.

Turn email verification on or off

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • Create or edit an appointment schedule.
  • Under “Booking form,” check or uncheck the “Require email verification” box.
  • Click Save.

Verify your email to you book an appointment

  • Fill out the booking form with your email address.
  • Check your email for a PIN.
  • On the booking page, enter the PIN.
  • Click Submit.

Share & manage calendars

Add a calendar to your website

You can embed your Google Calendar on your website to easily share your schedule and events with visitors.

Embed a calendar on your website

Important: Your calendar is only visible to people you share it with. To make your calendar visible to everyone, make your calendar public. Learn how to create and manage a public Google calendar.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
    • You can only get the embed code from a computer, not the Google Calendar app.
  • In the top right, click Settings menu Settings.
  • On the left side of the screen, click the name of the calendar you want to embed.
  • Click Integrate calendar.
  • Under “Embed code,” click Customize.
  • Choose options for your calendar display.
    • Select dimensions where the width and height are each at least 500.
    • If you turn off Navigation buttons, people can only use keyboard shortcuts to navigate your calendar.
  • At the top, next to the embed code, click Copy to clipboard .
  • On your website, paste the embed code. Tip: To subscribe to your embedded calendar, visitors can click Add to Google Calendar at the bottom left of the calendar.

Let site visitors save a calendar event

Important: To let anyone save your calendar event, make your calendar public. Learn how to create and manage a public Google calendar.

To allow anyone to save events to their calendars, add a Google Calendar button to your website.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
    • You can only make changes to your calendar visibility from a computer, not the mobile app.
  • On a public calendar, create or edit an event.
  • At the top right, click More actions Publish event.
  • In the “Publish event” window, copy the HTML code displayed.
  • On your website, paste the HTML code.

Get a public link for an event

To share a single event, get a public link for the event. Anyone with the link can get details about the event, like the time, date, and description.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

    • You can only get the event link from a computer, not the Google Calendar app.

  • On a public calendar, open the event you want to share.

  • At the top right, click More actions Publish event.

  • In the “Publish event” window, copy the link to share with others. Tips:

  • Links from the “Publish event” feature work best on desktop web browsers.

  • If you open these links in the Google Calendar app with a mobile device, the event details may not load correctly. It may also show as a blank event.

  • To check the event on a mobile device:

    • Open the link in a web browser like Chrome.
    • Save it to your calendar.

Related resource

Use keyboard shortcuts in Google Calendar

Choose what others can do with your calendar

Important: If you have a work or school account, your administrator can limit sharing or turn off permission settings. If you can’t select a setting for your calendar, contact your administrator.

After you share your calendar, you can set up permissions to control how others interact with your shared calendar. You can set permissions in Google Calendar for:

  • All events and tasks with start and end times on your calendar.
  • Specific people and groups.

Learn about Google Calendar access permissions

Permission

Definition

See only free/busy (hide details)

People can only find out when you’re busy on your calendar. They can’t find the names or details about events or tasks on your calendar.

See event details

People can find all details, like event names, times, places, and descriptions.

Make changes (see private events as free/busy)

People can edit non-private events, but can’t edit private events.

If a private event is “Busy,” it shows up as a busy block on your calendar with no event details.

If a private event is “Free,” it does not appear on your calendar.

Make changes and see event details

People can edit events on your calendar and find who else it’s shared with.

Make changes and manage sharing

People have full control of events or tasks with start and end time on your calendar and can share your calendar with others.

Broader calendar permissions take priority if there is a conflict in permission settings. For example:

  • You make your calendar public and set the permission to See event details.
  • Then, you share your calendar with someone else and set their permission to See only free/busy (hide details).
  • Since the calendar is public, anyone can find details about events on your calendar, including the person that has See only free/busy (hide details) access to your calendar.

Change permissions for your calendar

Important: The access permissions you choose for your calendar controls the behavior of visibility settings for individual events and tasks. Learn how to change event and task visibility on your calendar.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, find the “My calendars” section.
    • If you can’t find all your calendars, click My calendars.
  • To the right of the calendar, click More Settings and sharing.
  • On the left, click Access permissions for events.
  • Next to “Make available to public” or “Make available for {company name},” click the drop-down menu .
  • In the menu, select the permission.

Change permissions for specific people or groups

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left side of Calendar, click My calendars.
  • To the right of the calendar, click More Settings and sharing.
  • On the left, click Shared with.
  • Next to the person or group, click the drop-down menu .
  • In the menu, select the permission.

Learn how permissions affect what others can do on your calendar

Use the table below to check how your primary calendar’s permissions affect what others can do on your calendar.

What others can do

See free/busy

See event details

Make changes (see private events as free/busy)

Make changes and see event details

Make changes and manage sharing

View the calendar’s time zone

Subscribe to email alerts (for future events, creations, changes, cancellations, or responses)

See guest lists (for events without “See guest list” permission)

View sharing settings

Restore or permanently delete items from your trash

Create or edit events on your calendar

Update some details for tasks with time blocks (for example, “Do not disturb”)

Permanently delete the calendar

Create tasks on your calendar

  • As long as the event or task is not private.

  • Change event & task visibility on your calendar
  • Share your calendar
  • Create & manage a public Google calendar

Choose what others can do with your calendar (Computer)

Important: If you have a work or school account, your administrator can limit sharing or turn off permission settings. If you can’t select a setting for your calendar, contact your administrator.

After you share your calendar, you can set up permissions to control how others interact with your shared calendar. You can set permissions in Google Calendar for:

  • All events and tasks with start and end times on your calendar.
  • Specific people and groups.

Learn about Google Calendar access permissions

Permission

Definition

See only free/busy (hide details)

People can only find out when you’re busy on your calendar. They can’t find the names or details about events or tasks on your calendar.

See event details

People can find all details, like event names, times, places, and descriptions.

Make changes (see private events as free/busy)

People can edit non-private events, but can’t edit private events.

If a private event is “Busy,” it shows up as a busy block on your calendar with no event details.

If a private event is “Free,” it does not appear on your calendar.

Make changes and see event details

People can edit events on your calendar and find who else it’s shared with.

Make changes and manage sharing

People have full control of events or tasks with start and end time on your calendar and can share your calendar with others.

Broader calendar permissions take priority if there is a conflict in permission settings. For example:

  • You make your calendar public and set the permission to See event details.
  • Then, you share your calendar with someone else and set their permission to See only free/busy (hide details).
  • Since the calendar is public, anyone can find details about events on your calendar, including the person that has See only free/busy (hide details) access to your calendar.

Change permissions for your calendar

Important: The access permissions you choose for your calendar controls the behavior of visibility settings for individual events and tasks. Learn how to change event and task visibility on your calendar.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, find the “My calendars” section.
    • If you can’t find all your calendars, click My calendars.
  • To the right of the calendar, click More Settings and sharing.
  • On the left, click Access permissions for events.
  • Next to “Make available to public” or “Make available for {company name},” click the drop-down menu .
  • In the menu, select the permission.

Change permissions for specific people or groups

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left side of Calendar, click My calendars.
  • To the right of the calendar, click More Settings and sharing.
  • On the left, click Shared with.
  • Next to the person or group, click the drop-down menu .
  • In the menu, select the permission.

Learn how permissions affect what others can do on your calendar

Use the table below to check how your primary calendar’s permissions affect what others can do on your calendar.

What others can do

See free/busy

See event details

Make changes (see private events as free/busy)

Make changes and see event details

Make changes and manage sharing

View the calendar’s time zone

Subscribe to email alerts (for future events, creations, changes, cancellations, or responses)

See guest lists (for events without “See guest list” permission)

View sharing settings

Restore or permanently delete items from your trash

Create or edit events on your calendar

Update some details for tasks with time blocks (for example, “Do not disturb”)

Permanently delete the calendar

Create tasks on your calendar

  • As long as the event or task is not private.

Related resources

  • Change event & task visibility on your calendar
  • Share your calendar
  • Create & manage a public Google calendar

Choose what others can do with your calendar (iPhone and iPad)

Important: If you have a work or school account, your administrator can limit sharing or turn off permission settings. If you can’t select a setting for your calendar, contact your administrator.

After you share your calendar, you can set up permissions to control how others interact with your shared calendar. You can set permissions in Google Calendar for:

  • All events and tasks with start and end times on your calendar.
  • Specific people and groups.

Learn about Google Calendar access permissions

Permission

Definition

See only free/busy (hide details)

People can only find out when you’re busy on your calendar. They can’t find the names or details about events or tasks on your calendar.

People can find all details, like event names, times, places, and descriptions.

Change permissions for specific people or groups

Important: The access permissions you choose for your calendar controls the behavior of visibility settings for individual events and tasks. Learn how to change event and task visibility on your calendar.

  • On your iPhone or iPad, open the Calendar app .
  • At the top left, tap Menu Settings.
  • Select the calendar you want to share.
  • Under “Shared with,” tap the person or group.
  • In the menu, select the permission.

Delete or unsubscribe from a calendar

If you don’t need a calendar anymore, you can permanently delete it or unsubscribe from it. If you think you might need the calendar later, you can hide it instead.

Learn who can delete or unsubscribe from a calendar

  • As the calendar owner, you can:
    • Delete all events from your primary calendar. However, you can’t delete your primary calendar.
    • Delete any secondary calendars. If you share a calendar with other people or groups, the calendar is removed for everyone when you delete it.
  • If you’re the calendar owner, you can’t unsubscribe from a secondary calendar, but you can hide it.
    • To remove it, you must transfer ownership or delete it permanently. A secondary calendar is:
      • Any calendar that you create.
      • A group calendar that’s shared with you.
  • If you’re not the calendar owner, you can unsubscribe from a calendar to remove it from your list.

Options for removing calendars

Hide a calendar temporarily

This is a good option for calendars that you only check occasionally.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, find the calendar you want to hide. It may be under “My calendars” or “Other calendars.”
  • Click the calendar to hide or show it. Remove a calendar from your list temporarily

When you remove a calendar from your list, you won’t find it under “My calendars” or “Other calendars.”

Remove a calendar
  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, find the calendar you want to remove. It may be under “My calendars” or “Other calendars.”
  • Hover your mouse over the calendar name and click More .
  • Click Hide from list.
Bring back a calendar you removed
  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • In the left column, find the calendar you want to bring back.
  • Click Preview . Remove a calendar from your list permanently

Important: This action only applies to non-owners.

When you unsubscribe from a calendar, you won’t be able to find it again unless the calendar owner or someone with the “Make changes and manage sharing” permission re-shares it with you.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • In the left column, select the calendar you want to remove.
  • Click Remove calendar.
  • Click Unsubscribe.
  • Click Remove calendar. Restore a calendar you unsubscribed from

You can ask the calendar owner or someone with the “Make changes and manage sharing” permission to share the calendar with you again.

Step 1: Ask the owner to follow these steps
  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • In the left column, find the shared calendar you want to restore.
  • Click the name of the calendar.
  • Scroll down to the “Integrate calendar” section, and click the Secret or Public iCal address.
  • Copy the link and send it to the requester.
Step 2: Add the calendar in your account

When you receive the calendar link, you can add it again in your settings:

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • On the left, click Add calendar From URL.
  • Paste the Calendar Address link you received from the other person in the URL field.
  • Click Add Calendar. You should find the calendar listed on the left side of Google Calendar. Delete a secondary calendar

Important: This action is only available to the calendar owner.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • In the top right, click Settings Settings.
  • In the left column, find the calendar you want to delete.
  • Click the name of the calendar.
  • Click Remove calendar Delete Delete permanently. Delete all events in your primary calendar

You can’t delete your primary calendar, but you can delete all of its events.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top of the page, click Settings Settings.
  • Click the name of your primary calendar at the top of the list. It’s usually labeled as your first and last name.
  • At the bottom of the page, click Delete. It may take a few minutes to fully access Google Calendar’s features after clearing your primary calendar.

Related articles

  • Transfer calendars or events in Google Calendar
  • Share a calendar
  • Delete or remove an event

Find times to meet in Google Calendar

If someone shares their calendar with you, you can check their availability. You can also find times to meet.

Tip: If you have an eligible Google Workspace plan, you can use Gemini in Google Calendar to help you find a time to meet. Learn how to let Gemini in Google Calendar find times to meet.

Find someone else’s calendar

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • On the left, click Search for people.
  • Enter the name of the person.

Find a time when someone is available

Important: You can compare availability for up to 50 guests. If you add more than 50 guests to your event, only 50 schedules are shown.

If you need to check someone’s availability, compare the calendars of your guests. These guests must have shared their calendars with you. This helps you find an open time slot.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.

  • At the top left, click Create Event.

  • Add guests to your event.

  • Click Find a time.

  • Optional: To manage your view, click Filter and view :

    • Show or hide rooms: Click Show rooms.
    • Filter guests: Select No guests, Required guests, or All guests.

  • Find an available time on your calendar and your guests’ calendars. Tips:

  • To check if a room is available:

    • Click Add rooms or Add room booking.
    • Choose a room.

  • If a room is selected but you do not find its schedule, click Filter and view Show rooms.

Compare calendars in “Day” view

If you subscribe to someone’s calendar, you can check their schedule next to yours in your main calendar grid. This is a persistent setting that allows you to check each calendar in its own dedicated column rather than have them overlap.

  • On your computer, open Google Calendar.
  • At the top right, click Settings menu Settings.
  • On the left, click View options.
  • Check the box for “View calendars side by side in Day View”.
  • In your main calendar, make sure you are in “Day” view.

Learn what you need to find someone’s calendar

  • If the person’s calendar is shared publicly or within your organization, you can find their calendar.
  • If the person does not share their calendar with you, you can send them a request to share it. After they share their calendar, you get an email confirmation.
  • When people share their calendar, they can choose:
    • The information others can find about their events.
    • The permissions others have.
    • Learn how to change event visibility settings.

Related resources

  • Let Gemini in Google Calendar find times to meet
  • Change the visibility of events or tasks on your calendar
